<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49685253' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/09/11/y-combinators-garry-tan-wants-u-s-open-weight-ai-labs-to-distill-frontier-models-too/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Garry Tan wants US open-weight AI labs to &#x27;distill&#x27; frontier models, too</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Garry Tan 希望美国公开权重的 AI 实验室也参与对前沿模型进行蒸馏</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 08:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49685253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 411</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49685253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 236</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49685253'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Garry Tan argues that US open-weight AI labs should also distill frontier models to improve safety and accessibility, not just large incumbents. The idea envisions a more transparent, collaborative ecosystem around powerful AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Garry Tan 提议美国开放权重的 AI 实验室也应对前沿模型进行蒸馏，以提升安全性和可访问性，推动更为透明和协作的 AI 生态。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/garry-tan-sxsw-getty.jpg?resize=1200,801' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/garry-tan-sxsw-getty.jpg?resize=1200,801' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49693971' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://inflightsimulator.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I made a flight simulator, except you&#x27;re just a passenger</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：我做了一个飞行模拟器，你只是乘客</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 14, 2026 / 02:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49693971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 407</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49693971' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49693971'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Created a flight simulator where the user never controls the aircraft—you&#x27;re a passenger. It demonstrates a novel interaction model that emphasizes passenger experience, immersion, and simplified controls, challenging assumptions about what a flight sim can be. The idea could inspire new genres of simulation and accessibility-focused experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发了一款飞行模拟器，用户并不操作飞机，而是以乘客身份体验整段旅程。它展示了一种强调乘客体验、沉浸感与简化交互的新型玩法，挑战了人们对飞行模拟器的传统认识。此思路可能为新的模拟游戏类型与可访问性取向的体验提供灵感。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pirateface.co/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pirate Face Rescues LLM Models from Deletion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>海盗脸拯救 LLM 模型免于被删除</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 20, 2026 / 08:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49776699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 406</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49776699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 129</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49776699'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pirate Face reportedly salvaged several LLM models that were slated for deletion, illustrating how AI assets can disappear when hosts purge them. The move fuels debate about archiving, licensing, and who ultimately controls access to foundational AI resources.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称，海盗脸拯救了原本计划被删除的若干 LLM 模型，凸显在托管方清理时 AI 资产可能被抹去的问题。此举推动了关于归档、许可权以及谁掌控基础 AI 资源的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bensimms.moe/reverse-engineering-scooter/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reverse engineering my e-scooter and rewriting the firmware in Rust</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49638071' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 405</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49638071' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author reverse-engineers an e-scooter, analyzes its firmware, and rewrites it in Rust for safety and reliability. The project demonstrates practical embedded Rust workflows and the benefits of Rust for firmware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者对自己的电动滑板车进行了反向工程，分析固件并用 Rust 重写。项目展示了嵌入式 Rust 的实际工作流与提升安全性的可能性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49763928' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49763928' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/2026/09/18/if-math-is-more-than-proof-we-need-to-better-celebrate-the-rest-of-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If math is more than proof, we need to better celebrate the rest of it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果数学不仅是证明，我们应更好地庆祝它的其他方面</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 23:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49763928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 400</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49763928' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 279</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49763928'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Math rests beyond proof—intuition, examples, visualization, and community culture matter as much as formal rigour. Tao urges acknowledging these aspects to broaden participation and celebrate discovery, computation, and pedagogy in mathematics. This reframing matters for education and research funding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章主张，数学不仅仅是证明，直觉、直观示例、可视化和社区文化同样重要。作者呼吁重新定位对数学的庆祝对象，以促进参与度、普及教育与研究资助的多元化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/image-1.png' data-full='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/image-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49690174' data-tags='Hardware,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49690174' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.apple.com/accessories/dimensional-drawings/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple&#x27;s Dimensional Drawings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果的尺寸绘图</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 17:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49690174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 400</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49690174' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49690174'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple released dimensional drawings for its accessories, giving developers precise measurements needed to design compatible hardware. The move could spur more third‑party cases, docks, and accessories, while prompting considerations about IP and security implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果公开了配件的尺寸绘图，为开发者提供设计符合规格的精确尺寸。这一举措可能刺激更多第三方外设的开发，但也带来知识产权与安全方面的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://developer.apple.com/news/images/og/accessories-og.jpg' data-full='https://developer.apple.com/news/images/og/accessories-og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49737922' data-tags='AI,DevOps,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49737922' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://z.ai/blog/glm-built-its-inference-infrastructure' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How GLM built its own inference infrastructure</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM 构建了自己的推理基础设施</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 01:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49737922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 399</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49737922' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 277</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49737922'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM built its own inference infrastructure, signaling a move toward tighter integration between model development and serving. By owning the hardware and software stack, they aim to optimize latency, cost, and security, and reduce dependency on cloud providers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM 自建推理基础设施，体现了在模型开发与服务之间实现更紧密耦合的趋势。掌控硬件与软件栈有助于降低延迟、成本与安全风险，同时减少对云厂商的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://z-cdn-media.chatglm.cn/prompts-rich-media-resources/glm-built-its-inference-infrastructure/20260917-143951.jpeg?w=1376&amp;h=768&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://z-cdn-media.chatglm.cn/prompts-rich-media-resources/glm-built-its-inference-infrastructure/20260917-143951.jpeg?w=1376&amp;h=768&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49689048' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49689048' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://community.signalusers.org/t/registration-without-a-phone-number/2222?page=10' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Registration without a phone number on Signal will use zero-knowledge proofs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Signal 将在注册阶段无需手机号码，采用零知识证明</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 14:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49689048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 389</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49689048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 206</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49689048'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Signal is testing a phone-number-free registration using zero-knowledge proofs, letting users create accounts without sharing a phone number. This reduces SIM swap risk and privatizes identity, but introduces onboarding and trust challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Signal 正在测试无需手机号码的注册方式，利用零知识证明来实现去绑定身份。此举能提升隐私、降低 SIM 卡冒充风险，但在落地时需解决新用户引导与信任问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://community.signalusers.org/uploads/db3609/original/3X/2/a/2aae74b9e7c4af45dfcd09cfa6e9c90fb3b7534c.png' data-full='https://community.signalusers.org/uploads/db3609/original/3X/2/a/2aae74b9e7c4af45dfcd09cfa6e9c90fb3b7534c.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49763296' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://onionfutures.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>San Francisco Onion Futures Company</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>旧金山洋葱期货公司</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 21:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49763296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 386</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49763296' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49763296'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful project revisits onion futures by simulating trading for onions in San Francisco. It illuminates how futures markets work, including hedging, liquidity, and mispricing, even for everyday commodities. The exercise serves as a reminder of market design and the human factors behind it.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个富有趣味的项目通过在旧金山模拟洋葱期货交易，回顾期货市场的运作方式。它揭示了对冲、流动性与价格错配等要点，即便是日常商品也会受市场机制影响。此举强调市场设计及背后的人为因素。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.prinzai.com/p/gpt-6-astra-solves-a-wwi-german-radio' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-6 Astra Solves a WWI German Radio Cipher</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-6 Astra 破译一战德国无线电码</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 23:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49763987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 385</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49763987' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 175</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49763987'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GPT-6 Astra reportedly solves a WWI German radio cipher, illustrating that advanced LLMs can tackle historical cryptography tasks with structured reasoning. It underscores both the potential for AI-assisted cryptanalysis and the need to scrutinize reliability, reproducibility, and the scope of such capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称 GPT-6 Astra 解决了百年前德国无线电密码，显示先进的语言模型具备分步推理来应对历史性密码任务。该案例揭示 AI 协助密码分析的潜力，同时也提醒人们关注结果的可靠性、可重复性与适用范围。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.linuxfromscratch.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux from Scratch</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linux From Scratch 让你从源码逐步搭建一个 Linux 系统，洞悉内核、库和启动过程的每个环节。对于想要极简、可控且高度自定义的系统的开发者很有价值，但需要投入大量时间来维护。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49712278' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://withcapsule.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Capsule – Single-file web apps that save their data into SQLite</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Capsule——将数据保存到 SQLite 的单文件网页应用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 06:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49712278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 363</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49712278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49712278'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Capsule packages a web app as a single file that stores its data in SQLite, enabling offline-first apps with minimal footprints. This approach makes distribution and backups straightforward without a heavy server stack. It’s a lightweight path for portable, data-local web experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Capsule 将网页应用打包为单一文件，数据保存在 SQLite 中，实现离线优先、体积小的应用。此做法让分发与备份更简单，无需庞大的服务器栈。对于追求轻量、可移植的本地化网页体验非常有吸引力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://withcapsule.app/og-image.png' data-full='https://withcapsule.app/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 11:31 PT</span></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49744704'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new wild cat species has been described—the first in a century. The discovery underscores hidden biodiversity and the importance of habitat conservation amid rapid environmental change.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究团队描述了第一只在百年里被发现的新猫科物种，强调在生境现代化压力下仍存在未知生物多样性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49745570'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post critiques the hysteria around a hot tech trend, arguing for skepticism, rigorous testing, and clear benchmarks before shipping. It emphasizes separating signal from noise and prioritizing durable engineering practices over hype.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章抨击对某一热潮的狂热，主张保持怀疑、进行严格测试并设定明确基准，避免盲目跟风。强调在投入实际产品前需要分辨信息中的信号与噪声，并优先考虑长期稳健的工程实践。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49724927' data-tags='DevOps,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49724927' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gultsch.social/@daniel/117280438824908947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Google Play app review process now regularly takes longer than a week</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google Play 应用审核流程现在经常超过一周</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 04:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49724927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 360</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49724927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 344</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49724927'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google Play’s app review times have increasingly exceeded a week, delaying app updates and new launches. The growing backlog hurts developers&#x27; release cadence and can slow time-to-market for important features and security fixes. The trend highlights ongoing frictions in major app marketplaces as they scale and enforce security controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google Play 的应用审核时间现在经常超过一周，延缓了应用更新和新应用上线。这给开发者的发行节奏带来影响，更新推送成本上升，也可能促使他们寻求替代市场或其他分发方式。此趋势反映出大型应用商店在规模化与安全之间的持续摩擦。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gultsch.social/system/media_attachments/files/117/280/433/871/988/752/original/63774980a2cd31cf.png' data-full='https://gultsch.social/system/media_attachments/files/117/280/433/871/988/752/original/63774980a2cd31cf.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49676324' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49676324' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ToP9xfLDSME' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LG says we&#x27;re fake news [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LG：我们是假新闻[视频]</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 12, 2026 / 12:35 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一段视频显示 LG 声称相关报道是假新闻，凸显媒体叙事与企业信息在网络时代的张力。该剪辑反映品牌在面对新闻报道和社交视频时所面临的博弈。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://filipovski.net/2026/09/16/backups-arent-simple.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Backups Aren&#x27;t Simple</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49732513' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 351</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49732513' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 220</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49732513'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Backups Aren&#x27;t Simple argues that robust backup strategies require verification, retention policies, testing, and ransomware resilience, not just copying files. The post emphasizes guarding against data loss and ensuring reliable restores.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《备份并非简单》认为健全的备份策略需要核验、保留策略、定期测试和勒索软件防护，而不仅仅是简单地拷贝文件，强调确保可恢复性与数据安全。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.koreajoongangdaily.com/business/korea-raises-data-breach-fines-to-10-of-revenue/12869899' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Korea raises data breach fines to 10% of revenue</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>韩国将数据泄露罚金提高至年收10%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 13:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49759466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 333</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49759466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49759466'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>South Korea hikes data breach penalties to up to 10% of a company’s annual revenue, intensifying expectations for robust data security. The policy increases the potential cost of breaches for large firms and aligns with a global push toward stronger privacy enforcement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>韩国把数据泄露罚金提高到年收入的10%，以强化数据安全合规。此举使大型企业在数据事故中的潜在罚款大幅增加，推动企业加强隐私保护和信息安全投入。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The 5.4.0 release brings memory allocator improvements, including better fragmentation handling, new tuning knobs, and bug fixes that boost throughput in multi-core workloads. It’s a solid upgrade for latency-sensitive services and large-scale systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>5.4.0 版本在内存分配器上带来改进，包括更好的碎片处理、新增调优选项和若干 bug 修复，提升多核负载下的吞吐和延迟表现。对于对延迟敏感的服务和大型系统而言，这是一个值得关注的升级。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49718773'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author is deprecating a tiny PHP fix they shipped in 2014 that somehow gained 20 million installs. The post reflects on legacy tech debt, the fragility of tiny abstractions in the wild, and the need for explicit maintenance or sunset plans.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.liampwll.com/posts/bend_vibe_coding/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bend 2 and the Vibe-Coding Trap</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bend 2 与氛围编码陷阱</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 05:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49753179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 320</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49753179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 232</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49753179'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece critiques the &#x27;vibe coding&#x27; culture, where decisions are driven by mood or trends rather than solid design. It argues this trap harms maintainability and reliability, and urges grounding choices in metrics and disciplined review.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中批评“氛围编码”文化——以情绪或潮流驱动决策而非扎实设计。指出此陷阱会损害可维护性与可靠性，倡导以度量与严格评审来支撑技术选择。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国驾照信息泄露被视作国家安全灾难</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 08:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49714547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 320</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49714547' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 201</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49714547'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A driver’s license data breach is framed as a national-security disaster due to identity theft risks and impacts on government services. The piece argues for urgent reforms to data protection and credentialing infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>驾照信息泄露被视为国家安全灾难，原因是身份盗用风险及对政府服务的影响。文中呼吁对数据保护与凭证基础设施进行紧急改革。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49727627' data-tags='Open Source,Culture,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49727627' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://frvr.com/blog/news/ps5-linux-lead-quits-as-open-source-projects-have-become-a-bunch-of-noobs-using-llms-that-they-dont-even-understand/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PS5 Linux lead quits: &quot;a bunch of noobs using LLMs&quot; that &quot;they don&#x27;t understand&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PS5 Linux 项目负责人辞职：’一群不懂却在用大型语言模型的菜鸟’</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 07:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49727627' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 320</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49727627' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 220</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49727627'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The PS5 Linux lead has quit amid a clash over governance and culture in open-source development, branding critics as &#x27;a bunch of noobs using LLMs they don&#x27;t understand.&#x27; The resignation spotlights tension between seasoned maintainers and AI-powered tooling, raising questions about project direction, sustainability, and community governance. It also signals how AI tools are reshaping collaboration in high-profile OSS projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PS5 Linux 项目负责人离职，指责社区成员在使用大型语言模型时缺乏理解。事件暴露开放源代码项目在治理、AI 工具依赖与社区贡献方式上的冲突。它也揭示了 AI 工具正在重塑高知名度开源项目的协作方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49778029' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49778029' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.sedaily.com/finance/2026/09/20/samsung-to-double-hbm4-output-next-year-sources-say' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Samsung is expected to more than double output of its HBM4 and HBM4E DRAM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三星HBM4/HBM4E DRAM产量有望翻倍以上</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 20, 2026 / 10:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49778029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 314</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49778029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 201</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49778029'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Samsung is reportedly set to more than double the output of its HBM4 and HBM4E DRAM next year to meet surging AI and data-center demand. The planned capacity ramp could help ease tight supply for high-bandwidth memory used in GPUs and accelerators, potentially impacting pricing and supply chains across hyperscalers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>三星计划在来年把 HBM4 与 HBM4E DRAM 的产量大幅提升，超过现有水平的两倍，以满足 AI 与数据中心对高带宽内存的需求。这一扩产将缓解相关供需紧张，可能影响 GPU/加速器市场的定价与供应。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wimg.sedaily.com/news/cms/2026/09/20/rcv.YNA.20260318.PYH2026031803840001300_P1.jpg' data-full='https://wimg.sedaily.com/news/cms/2026/09/20/rcv.YNA.20260318.PYH2026031803840001300_P1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bw.swerdlow.dev/report' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Brood War Bench</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 07:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49766966' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 314</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49766966' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 138</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A benchmarking report for StarCraft: Brood War, detailing performance metrics, potential bottlenecks, and reproducible tests on modern hardware. The study provides insights for developers exploring AI agents and engine behavior in legacy games. Takeaway: rigorous benchmarks help optimize even classic engines on new hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《星际争霸：虫群之心》基准测试报告给出性能指标、潜在瓶颈与可复现实验方法，聚焦在现代硬件上的表现。研究为探索在遗留游戏中的 AI 代理和引擎行为的开发者提供了洞见。要点是，通过严格的基准测试，可以在新硬件上为经典引擎找到优化方向。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49752614'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Warren Buffett steps down as Berkshire Hathaway chairman and designates his son to take over. The move signals succession planning in a family-led empire and tests how Berkshire maintains its long-term value strategy under new leadership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>沃伦·巴菲特辞去伯克希尔董事长职务并指定儿子接任，标志着家族掌控企业的接班计划。此举将检验在新领导下，伯克希尔能否继续维持长期价值投资的策略。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reutersconnect.com/item/israeli-minister-threatens-filmmakers-citizenship-over-gaza-documentary/dGFnOnJldXRlcnMuY29tLDIwMjY6bmV3c21sX09XQ1ZNQzQ0NjcxMC1WSURFTw' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Israeli Minister Threatens Filmmakers&#x27; Citizenship over Gaza Documentary</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>以色列部长威胁剥夺加沙纪录片制片人公民身份</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 07:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49713468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 308</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49713468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49713468'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An Israeli minister has threatened to strip citizenship from filmmakers behind a Gaza documentary, signaling aggressive government leverage over media projects tied to the conflict. The move underscores how official power can intersect with storytelling, potentially chilling journalists and creatives who cover sensitive subjects. This raises questions about freedom of expression and the balance between national security and press independence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以色列一位部长威胁要取消一部加沙纪录片幕后制片人的公民身份，显示政府对相关报道的强硬态度。此举暴露出政治因素对制片选题和记者生计的直接影响，引发对言论自由和新闻自由的担忧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 19:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49691343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 308</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49691343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 426</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nike&#x27;s exit from the S&amp;P 100 after 18 years comes amid a dramatic decline in market cap, triggering index rebalancing. The move underscores how corporate value volatility can outpace index membership and affect passive investors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>耐克在18年后退出标普100，伴随市值大幅下滑，引发指数成分调整。此举凸显企业价值的波动可能超越指数成分的变动，对被动投资者产生影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://minimalcompany.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Minimal Phone 2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Minimal Phone 2 极简手机二代</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 19:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49749369' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 306</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49749369' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49749369'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Minimal Phone 2 continues the trend of privacy-first smartphones with a pared-down OS and minimal data collection. It trades broad app ecosystems for user control and reduced risk of surveillance, appealing to privacy-conscious users and developers exploring alternative mobile paradigms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Minimal Phone 2 延续了隐私优先、极简设计的手机路线，减小预装应用和数据跟踪。它以更高的用户控制和较低的监控风险吸引隐私关注者，代表了对主流生态的一种替代选择。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://skywriter.blue/@segyges.bsky.social/3mvom4b4dn22q' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI safety is mostly a sex cult</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 安全其实基本上就是个性崇拜团体</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 01:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49737985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 305</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49737985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 247</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49737985'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that AI safety discourse is dominated by a tight-knit, gatekept circle that enforces norms through social dynamics. It calls for more open, diverse governance and less dogmatic adherence to a single worldview.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 AI 安全领域被一个紧密封闭的圈子主导，通过社会压力和门槛来维护规范。呼吁建立更开放、多元且透明的治理路径，减少教条化影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/dotnet/performance-improvements-in-net-11/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Performance Improvements in .NET 11</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>.NET 11 的性能改进</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 05:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49711424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 301</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49711424' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49711424'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>.NET 11 performance improvements detail faster startup, reduced allocations, and GC/JIT enhancements that boost throughput for server and consumer workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>.NET 11 性能改进包括更快的启动、减少分配以及垃圾回收和即时编译的改进，提升服务器和客户端工作负载的吞吐量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/dotnet/wp-content/uploads/sites/10/2026/09/net11perf.webp' data-full='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/dotnet/wp-content/uploads/sites/10/2026/09/net11perf.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49704246' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dbtcharts.com/blog/charts-built-for-chat/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Charts built for Chat</a></p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49750094' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fex-emu.com/Scourge-of-emulation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The scourge of x86 emulation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>x86 仿真的祸根</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 21:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49750094' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 281</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49750094' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49750094'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>x86 emulation on non-x86 architectures continues to suffer from performance penalties, fidelity gaps, and security quirks. The piece argues that while emulation enables cross-platform compatibility, practical tradeoffs demand careful optimization and disclosure of edge cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在非 x86 架构上进行 x86 仿真仍存在性能损失、准确性差异和安全特性偏差等问题。文章指出，仿真虽然带来跨平台兼容性，但实际权衡需要对边界情况进行充分优化与披露。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.com/FEX-Emu/fex-emu.com/raw/main/images/fex-textlogo-140h.png' data-full='https://github.com/FEX-Emu/fex-emu.com/raw/main/images/fex-textlogo-140h.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49735930' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49735930' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ryan.science/blog/keys-not-included' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Keys Not Included: recovering the signing keys for US driver&#x27;s license barcodes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>未包含密钥：恢复美国驾照条码的签名密钥</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 20:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49735930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 281</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49735930' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 149</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49735930'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece dives into the process of recovering signing keys used to sign US driver&#x27;s license barcodes, highlighting key management gaps and potential security implications for ID integrity. It explains the challenges of reconstituting cryptographic keys without original materials and what it means for barcode validation and revocation workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文聚焦于用于签署美国驾照条码数据的密钥如何被找回，突出密钥管理缺口及其对身份认证完整性的潜在影响。文章描述在没有原始材料的情况下重建密钥的难点，以及这对条码校验与密钥撤销流程的意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ryan.science/tessa.png' data-full='https://ryan.science/tessa.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49725356' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/video-games/playstation/26-year-old-sony-ps2-security-chip-broken-wide-open-after-four-years-of-effort-reverse-engineering-enthusiast-successfully-unlocks-cxp102064-mechacon-chip' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Original Sony PlayStation 2 security chip &#x27;broken wide open&#x27; after 26 years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>索尼PlayStation 2 原始安全芯片在26年后被彻底破解</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 04:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49725356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 280</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49725356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49725356'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A long-running reverse-engineering effort finally cracked the PS2&#x27;s security chip, shedding light on how the system prevents tampering. The breakthrough exposes security flaws that persisted for decades and has implications for hardware preservation, emulation, and security nostalgia. It demonstrates how even enduring hardware protections can eventually yield to deep analysis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>经过长期逆向工程的努力，PS2 的安全芯片终于被破解，揭示了系统防篡改的原理。此次突破暴露了数十年来持续存在的安全漏洞，并对硬件保存、仿真与安全研究产生影响。也显示出即便是长期的硬件保护，最终也会被深入分析所突破。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/YT8szWTawNgyAr5wNtX4gh-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/YT8szWTawNgyAr5wNtX4gh-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 14:07 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rheinmetall has released the protocol for its Battlesuite connected weapons stack as open source. The move highlights a rare instance of transparency in military tech and could spur cross-vendor interoperability and defense software ecosystems, while raising safety and dual-use concerns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>莱茵金属将其 Battlesuite 连接武器系统协议开源，此举在军事科技领域并不常见。该动作可能推动跨厂商互操作性与防务软件生态，但也引发安全和双用途应用的担忧。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49740507' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cwly7vkke4jxo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Canada welcomes EU proposal to become &#x27;associate member&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加拿大欢迎欧盟提出的准成员地位倡议</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 06:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49740507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49740507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 343</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49740507'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canada endorses a proposal to deepen ties with the EU by adopting an &#x27;associate member&#x27; status. Such a status would streamline regulatory alignment, trade, and security cooperation, potentially reshaping tech policy and data flows between North America and the EU. The move signals Canada’s readiness to formalize a closer, more structured partnership beyond traditional trade pacts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加拿大支持通过准成员身份深化与欧盟的关系，可能推动监管、贸易与数据流动方面的更紧密协作。此举有望重塑北美与欧盟之间的科技政策与数据治理格局，并显示加拿大愿意在传统贸易框架之外形成更结构化的伙伴关系。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/bbbd/live/12d00980-b28e-11f1-8096-b33a1297275a.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/bbbd/live/12d00980-b28e-11f1-8096-b33a1297275a.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://status.salesforce.com/products/all' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Salesforce Global Outage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Salesforce 全球宕机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 03:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49724488' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 274</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49724488' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 181</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49724488'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Salesforce suffered a global outage that disrupted access to core CRM and cloud services across regions. Incident responders are investigating the root cause and restoring services, with status updates guiding customers through recovery. The outage underscores the risk of SaaS dependencies for business operations and the importance of incident readiness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Salesforce 遭遇全球性宕机，影响了多区域的核心 CRM 与云服务。故障原因正在调查，服务逐步恢复，状态更新帮助客户度过修复阶段。此次事件凸显 SaaS 依赖对业务运营的风险，以及完善应急准备的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/09/12/automattic-confirms-mullenweg-has-returned-as-ceo-after-attempted-ouster-by-board/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mullenweg has returned as CEO after attempted board ouster</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 13:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49688259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 274</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49688259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 378</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49688259'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Automattic founder Matt Mullenweg has resumed CEO duties after a board attempt to oust him, signaling a stabilization of governance. The move will influence product strategy across WordPress.com and related open-source initiatives, and tests how corporate governance works in a distributed, creator-led company.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Automattic 创始人 Matt Mullenweg 在董事会试图罢黜后重新担任 CEO，标志着治理局势的稳定。此举将影响 WordPress.com 等产品路线和开源计划的走向，并考验在分布式、以创作者为导向的公司中的治理实践。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49690554' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49690554' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://giannirosato.com/blog/post/case-against-jxl/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The case against JPEG XL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JPEG XL 的反对理由</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 18:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49690554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 273</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49690554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 370</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49690554'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues against adopting JPEG XL, citing adoption friction, limited ecosystem support, and potential licensing concerns. It suggests sticking with more established formats (like AVIF or WebP) that enjoy broader tooling and compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章从采用障碍、生态支持不足以及潜在许可问题等角度，提出反对 JPEG XL 的观点。作者建议在现有工具链中优先选择更成熟、兼容性更高的格式，如 AVIF 或 WebP。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49755024' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://overreacted.io/how-i-vibed-a-proof-of-conways-conjecture/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I vibed a proof of Conway&#x27;s conjecture</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我对 Conway 猜想的证明有灵感</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 07:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49755024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 252</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49755024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 256</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49755024'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal account discusses a heuristic intuition that led toward a proof of Conway&#x27;s conjecture, reflecting on how vibes and rigorous reasoning interact. It emphasizes that intuition can guide discovery but must be validated through formal proof.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者分享自己对 Conway 猜想的直觉启发，反思直觉与严格推理之间的关系。强调直觉可以指引发现，但需要通过正式的证明来验证。这一讨论也凸显了数学进展中的社会性与协作要素。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Large language models are changing how people learn to code, but fundamentals still matter. <p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16338' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Breaking the 1.58-bit Barrier for Ternary LLMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>打破三值LLM的1.58比特瓶颈</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 13:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49732931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 241</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49732931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49732931'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers break the 1.58-bit barrier for ternary LLMs, suggesting that highly quantized, three-value models can still achieve competitive performance. The work highlights new techniques for maintaining accuracy at ultra-low bitwidth, enabling cheaper, more scalable inference.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究团队打破三值语言模型的1.58比特瓶颈，表明极低位宽的三值权重也能达到可观的性能。该工作展示了在极低位宽下保持准确度的新办法，使推理成本更低、扩展性更强。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49727580' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mustafa-suleyman.ai/a-warning-about-model-welfare' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A warning about &#x27;model welfare&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于“模型福利”的警告</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 07:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49727580' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 234</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49727580' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 646</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49727580'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mustafa Suleyman cautions that as AI models scale, we risk treating them as moral actors with welfare concerns. He argues for governance that addresses welfare-related concerns without anthropomorphizing models, and for safeguards against misaligned incentives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mustafa Suleyman 警示，随着模型能力提升，我们可能对其福利产生拟人化的伦理担忧。他主张在不将模型拟人化的前提下，制定覆盖福利相关风险的治理框架，并防范错位激励。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mustafa-suleyman.ai/images/posts/a-warning-about-model-welfare.jpg' data-full='https://mustafa-suleyman.ai/images/posts/a-warning-about-model-welfare.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49715113'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative article argues there is a 100% chance AI agents are already ruining the internet, by automating content creation, gaming systems, and eroding trust. It discusses how autonomous agents influence search results, moderation, and information quality, potentially magnifying misinformation and manipulation. The piece serves as a warning about designing AI-powered systems with safeguards to preserve the integrity of online ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇挑衅性的观点文章声称，AI代理人已经在毁坏互联网，原因包括自动化内容创作、系统操控和信任下降。文章讨论自治代理如何影响搜索、内容审核和信息质量，可能放大错误信息与操纵的风险。作者警告在设计 AI 系统时需要加入防护措施，以维护在线生态的完整性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lawandcrime.com/high-profile/the-government-was-entitled-trumps-border-agents-can-now-search-cellphones-without-a-warrant-probable-cause-or-reasonable-suspicion-2nd-circuit-rules/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Border agents can search cellphones without a warrant or reasonable suspicion</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>边境执法人员无需搜查令或合理怀疑即可搜查手机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 11:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49758028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 229</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49758028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 186</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49758028'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A court ruling extends border search powers, allowing agents to search cellphones without a warrant or probable cause. The decision has sweeping privacy implications for travelers at border crossings and raises ongoing Fourth Amendment debates about government authority versus individual rights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>二审法院裁定边境人员在无搜查令、无合理怀疑的情况下也可搜查手机。这一判定极大地扩张了边境执法的权限，也引发对第四修正案隐私保护的广泛讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX documents a design-and-manufacturing streamlining of the Raptor engine, reducing part count and simplifying assembly. The streamlining aims to lower cost, improve reliability, and accelerate iteration cycles for propulsion tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX 记录了对 Raptor 引擎的设计与制造精简，降低部件数量、简化组装流程，力求降低成本、提升可靠性并缩短推进技术的迭代周期。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Martin Fowler argues that despite hype, LLMs have serious shortcomings in reliability, interpretability, and safety. He advocates pragmatic use cases, guardrails, and clearly defined boundaries for deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>马丁·福勒指出，尽管热度高涨，大型语言模型在可靠性、可解释性与安全性方面存在严重短板。文章倡导务实的应用场景、 guardrails 以及明确的部署边界。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-09-whales-stillborn-humpback-whale-grieving.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Weeping whales: Stillborn humpback whale grieving documented</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>鲸群哀悼：记录到死产座头鲸的悲痛</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 18:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49735159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 224</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49735159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 164</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49735159'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers documented a stillborn humpback calf and observed apparent grieving within the mother and pod. The finding adds to evidence of complex social emotions in whales and informs conservation and welfare considerations for marine mammals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员记录了一例死产座头鲸并观察到母鲸及群体的哀悼迹象。这一发现为鲸类的复杂社会情感提供了更多证据，并对海洋哺乳动物的保护与福利提出关注。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49693610'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EuroBirdPortal aggregates live bird-tracking data across Europe into an interactive map. It highlights migration routes, seasonal hotspots, and species movement patterns, offering a valuable resource for researchers and conservationists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该平台把欧洲多源鸟类追踪数据汇聚成交互地图，展示迁徙路线、季节性热点和物种运动模式。为研究者与保护工作者提供宝贵的数据资源。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>朝鲜核试验引发多年地震</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 07:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49755160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 223</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49755160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 179</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49755160'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Seismic monitoring shows a North Korean nuclear test triggered aftershocks and persistent tremor sequences lasting years. The event demonstrates how underground tests leave long-lasting seismic footprints, informing monitoring efforts and verification. It also highlights challenges in attribution with evolving sensor networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>地震监测显示，朝鲜核试验引发了持续多年的余震和长期震动序列。这一现象揭示地下试验会留下持久的地震印记，为监测和核查工作提供证据，同时也凸显在传感网络不断升级的背景下对来源归属的挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://harnesstax.github.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HarnessTax: How Much Does the Harness Matter for Coding Agents?</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 15:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49733726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 221</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49733726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49733726'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HarnessTax investigates how much the coding agent harness influences performance, reliability, and behavior in autonomous tooling. The write-up discusses empirical findings, tradeoffs of harness complexity, and guidance for choosing or building an effective agent framework.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HarnessTax 探讨在编码代理中，Harness 对性能、可靠性和行为的影响有多大。文中给出经验发现、对复杂性取舍的分析，以及在选择或构建高效代理框架时的建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://planetscale.com/blog/introducing-tin' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tin: full-text search for Postgres</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tin：Postgres 的全文检索</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 06:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49766611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 220</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49766611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 86</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49766611'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tin adds full-text search to Postgres as a native layer from PlanetScale, aiming to deliver fast indexing and natural-language queries within the Postgres ecosystem. It emphasizes staying inside Postgres to simplify deployment and avoid external search services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PlanetScale 推出 Tin，为 Postgres 提供原生的全文搜索能力，目标是在 Postgres 生态中实现快速索引和自然语言查询。它强调保持在 Postgres 内部以简化部署，避免依赖外部搜索服务。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49734467' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://medium.com/@TmlrOrg/asking-authors-about-their-own-papers-3d2e04e5dee0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Asking authors about their own papers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>向作者询问他们自己的论文</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 16:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49734467' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49734467' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49734467'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Asking Authors About Their Own Papers explores the value of directly querying authors to clarify, contextualize, and supplement published work. The approach can improve understanding, reproducibility, and surface insights not captured in the text.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《向作者询问他们自己的论文》探讨直接联系作者以获取论文背景和澄清的做法，强调这样可以提升理解与可重复性，并揭示文本未覆盖的洞见。通过作者视角补充信息，有助于读者更全面地把握研究成果。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://miro.medium.com/v2/da:true/bc1f8416df0cad099e43cda2872716e5864f18a73bda2a7547ea082aca9b5632' data-full='https://miro.medium.com/v2/da:true/bc1f8416df0cad099e43cda2872716e5864f18a73bda2a7547ea082aca9b5632' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49757050' data-tags='Security,Hardware,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49757050' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://donjon.ledger.com/blog/rp2350-secure-debug-laser-fault-injection/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Photon-Emission-Guided Laser Fault Injection Enables RP2350 Secure Debug</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>光子发射引导的激光故障注入让 RP2350 实现安全调试</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 09:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49757050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 217</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49757050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49757050'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Photon-emission-guided laser fault injection enables secure debugging on RP2350 hardware by exploiting precisely timed laser pulses guided by optical emissions. This demonstrates a new class of side-channel vulnerabilities and the cat-and-mouse game between hardware security and attackers. The result reinforces the need for robust hardware tamper-resistance and secure debug protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过光子发射引导的激光故障注入，研究者能够在 RP2350 硬件上实现安全调试，利用对光学发射信号的时序引导来触发故障。这揭示了一类新的旁路攻击风险，也突显了软硬件防护与攻击之间的博弈。此结果强调加强硬件防篡改与安全调试保护的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://donjon.ledger.com/blog/rp2350-secure-debug-laser-fault-injection/cover.png' data-full='https://donjon.ledger.com/blog/rp2350-secure-debug-laser-fault-injection/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49674451' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49674451' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gegell.github.io/posts/factorio-rng/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reversing Factorio&#x27;s RNG</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Factorio的 RNG 逆向工程</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 12, 2026 / 10:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49674451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49674451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49674451'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post details reverse engineering Factorio&#x27;s RNG to understand how randomness shapes gameplay, revealing potential seeds or patterns. It illustrates how even well-balanced games can have predictable elements, informing modders and researchers about RNG design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章对 Factorio 的 RNG 进行逆向分析，以理解随机性对游戏玩法的影响，揭示潜在的种子或模式。它展示了即使是平衡性极高的游戏也可能存在可预测的元素，为 Mod 制作者与研究者提供了对 RNG 设计的洞见。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gegell.github.io/_app/immutable/assets/factorio-breaking-rng-thumbnail.BkTOyfMu.jpg' data-full='https://gegell.github.io/_app/immutable/assets/factorio-breaking-rng-thumbnail.BkTOyfMu.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49704008' data-tags='Legal,Politics,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49704008' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://law.justia.com/cases/federal/appellate-courts/ca9/26-1444/26-1444-2026-08-04.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amazon vs. Perplexity – U.S. Court of Appeals for the Ninth Circuit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚马逊对 Perplexity：美国第九巡回上诉法院</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 14, 2026 / 14:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49704008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 216</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49704008' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 217</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49704008'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Ninth Circuit ruling involving Amazon and the AI service Perplexity could shape how platforms handle AI-sourced content and user-generated material. The decision signals evolving liability frameworks for AI-assisted services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>涉及亚马逊与 Perplexity 的第九巡回法院裁决或将影响平台对 AI 获取信息来源和用户生成内容的责任界定。裁决反映出对 AI 助手服务的责任框架正在演化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49753878' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49753878' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An empirical study of harness design for coding agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于编码代理的控制框架设计的实证研究</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 06:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49753878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 214</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49753878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49753878'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The study benchmarks different control-harness designs for coding agents, examining how interfaces shape behavior, safety, and reliability. It provides practical guidance for building more predictable AI coding assistants and highlights challenges in evaluating such systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这项实证研究比较了多种用于编码代理的控制框架设计，揭示不同接口如何影响代理行为、安全性与可预测性。结果为搭建更可靠的AI编码助手提供具体建议，同时也揭示评估此类系统的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://archive.org/details/b904a8eb-9c98-4bb1-bf25-3cb9d075b157' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Warez: The Infrastructure and Aesthetics of Piracy (2021)</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 19:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49749724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 213</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49749724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49749724'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Warez is a 2021 look at the infrastructure and aesthetics of piracy, analyzing how pirate ecosystems are organized and communicated. The book offers historical and cultural context for digital piracy and illicit distribution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这本 2021 年出版的书深入考察 piracy 的基础设施与美学，分析盗版生态的技术、组织与文化维度，以及它对数字版权、网络治理的启示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rh1tech/frank-386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 386 PC for Your RP2350</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 386 PC for Your RP2350 shows a project that emulates a 386-era PC on the RP2350 platform, enabling retro software and educational demos on a microcontroller-board. It demonstrates how hobbyists squeeze legacy computing into tiny devices and what it implies for embedded systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这个在 RP2350 上实现的 386 电脑项目，可以在微控制器板上运行 386 时代的软件，适合教学演示和复古计算爱好者实验。展示了爱好者如何把遗留计算嵌入到小型设备中，并对嵌入式系统的潜力提供了启示。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49746117'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Japan is aging rapidly, with centenarians surpassing 100,000. The shift pressures healthcare, pensions, and labor markets, accelerating interest in robotics, remote care, and age-friendly tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本人口快速老龄化，百岁及以上人口已突破十万。此趋势对医疗、养老金与劳动市场带来压力，也推动机器人、远程护理与适老科技的加速发展。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49771966' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://saweis.net/posts/rsa-896.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RSA-896</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RSA-896：大模数的因数分解进展</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 19:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49771966' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 208</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49771966' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49771966'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report on factoring RSA-896, an 896-bit RSA modulus, illustrating advances in factorization techniques and distributed computing. The result underscores the ongoing pressure to move to larger key sizes (2048 bits or more) to maintain cryptographic security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于对 RSA-896（896 位模数）的因数分解进展，展示因数分解技术与分布式计算的提升。这一成果再次提醒业界需要采用更大密钥长度（2048 位及以上）以维持密码系统安全。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49726955' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
