<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dream-RSI: Recursive Self-Improvement through Evolving Worlds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dream-RSI：通过演化世界实现递归自我提升</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 06:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49726955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 207</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49726955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49726955'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study on Dream-RSI outlines recursive self-improvement by training agents in evolving environments. The approach explores how iterative self-improvement loops could scale AI capabilities, while underscoring safety and alignment challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dream-RSI 的研究探讨在不断演化的环境中训练代理以实现递归自我提升。该思路揭示自我改进循环如何潜在放大AI能力，同时也强调安全性与对齐问题的挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49777841'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The policy reversal loosens emissions limits for power plants, potentially increasing climate pollution. The move affects climate policy, regulation, and the pace at which cleaner energy and tech solutions can scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>政策取消了对发电厂气候污染的限制，可能导致更高的排放并影响环境监管。此举对气候科技、监管不确定性及清洁能源部署速度具有重要影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让初创企业变得更强大</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 07:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684196' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 202</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684196' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49684196'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Paul Graham argues that startups should focus on building leverage and durable growth to become truly powerful, not just fast iterations. The piece offers practical guidance on focus, sequencing, and scaling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>保罗·格雷厄姆主张创业公司应追求可持续的影响力与杠杆效应，而不仅是短期扩张。文章给出创始人应关注的优先级、资源分配与扩张策略。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49761432' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49761432' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/llms-for-chip-design' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How OpenAI Used Its Own LLMs to Design Its Jalapeño Chip</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 如何用自家 LLM 设计 Jalapeño 芯片</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 16:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49761432' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49761432' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49761432'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI reportedly used its own large language models to assist in the design of a Jalapeño chip, illustrating a practical use of LLMs in hardware design workflows. The approach likely combines generative modeling with optimization loops to explore geometries or architectures, highlighting how AI can accelerate engineering prototyping. The case raises questions about reliability, reproducibility, and the boundary between AI-assisted design and traditional EDA tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，OpenAI 使用自家的大语言模型参与 Jalapeño 芯片的设计，展示了 LLM 在硬件设计流程中的实际应用。该方法可能将生成建模与优化循环结合，用于探索几何结构或架构，体现 AI 如何加速原型开发。此事也引发对可靠性、可重复性以及 AI 辅助设计与传统 EDA 工具边界的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dailyneuron.com/aphantasia-mental-imagery-brain-network/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>People who can&#x27;t picture anything are rewriting the science of imagination</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无法在脑海中想象画面的群体正在重塑想象力科学</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 14, 2026 / 06:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49696453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 200</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49696453' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 283</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49696453'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings on aphantasia suggest some people cannot conjure mental images yet still engage in imaginative thought, prompting a rethink of how imagination maps to brain networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于无图像想象（失像症）的最新研究显示，部分人无法在脑海中形成画面却仍能进行想象，引发对想象力如何映射到脑网络的新思考。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.chesterwisniewski.com/post/2026-09-13-ai-is-destroying-the-creative-commons/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI and the Destruction of the Creative Commons</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 與 Creative Commons 的破坏</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 20, 2026 / 03:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49774329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 200</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49774329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 219</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49774329'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that training AI on copyrighted and Creative Commons works undermines open licenses and creators’ rights, fueling calls for clearer data provenance and license reforms. It highlights potential long-term risks to the viability of open licensing in AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为将 AI 训练数据来自于受版权保护的作品和 Creative Commons 许可，会削弱开放许可与创作者权益，推动对数据来源与许可改革的呼声，并警示 AI 开放许可未来可能面临的风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章强调放弃深度思考从来不是解决办法，依赖自动化也并非万无一失。要在复杂任务中使用AI时保持批判性思维、建立正确的认知模型并进行持续评估。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49734929' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49734929' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gcaptain.com/the-return-of-sail-power-cargo-ships-are-turning-back-to-the-wind/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Return of Sail Power: Cargo Ships Are Turning Back to the Wind</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>帆力回归：货船回归风力航运</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 17:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49734929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49734929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49734929'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cargo ships are reintroducing wind-powered or wind-assisted propulsion to cut fuel costs and emissions. The shift highlights decarbonization efforts in global logistics, while presenting technical and economic hurdles to wide adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>货运船舶重新采用风力驱动或风力辅助推进，以降低燃料成本和排放。这一转变凸显全球物流的 decarbonization 努力，同时也暴露了大规模应用的技术与经济挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gcaptain.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Maersk-Container-Anemoi-Rotor-Sail-6-scaled.png.jpeg' data-full='https://gcaptain.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Maersk-Container-Anemoi-Rotor-Sail-6-scaled.png.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49719938' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nand2mario.github.io/posts/2026/zsst-voodoo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Recreating Voodoo Graphics and a Late-1990s Gaming PC on an FPGA</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 FPGA 上重现 Voodoo 图形与九十年代末的游戏 PC</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 15:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49719938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 188</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49719938' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49719938'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project recreates the Voodoo Graphics pipeline and a late-1990s PC on an FPGA, illustrating retro hardware emulation and the trade-offs between accuracy and engineering practicality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 FPGA 上重现 Voodoo 图形管线和九十年代末一台游戏 PC，展示了对复古硬件的仿真与保留。讨论实现中的时序挑战、精度取舍，以及对数字保存的启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nand2mario.github.io/posts/2026/zsst-voodoo/teapot.png' data-full='https://nand2mario.github.io/posts/2026/zsst-voodoo/teapot.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49715934' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Data,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49715934' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dallincrump.com/dystopian-surveillance-is-becoming-a-reality' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dystopian Surveillance Is Becoming a Reality</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>反乌托邦式监控正成为现实</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 10:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49715934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 185</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49715934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49715934'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pervasive surveillance tech—from cameras to data analytics—has moved from fiction to routine reality. The piece explains how collectors and regulators deploy profiling, location tracking, and behavioral analytics to shape society. It warns that without strong safeguards, civil liberties could shrink under the weight of omnipresent monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>普及的监控技术从虚构走向日常现实，包括摄像头和数据分析在内的手段正在塑造社会。文章阐述了监控者与监管机构如何利用画像、位置跟踪和行为分析来影响社会秩序。若缺乏强有力的防护措施，公民自由可能在无处不在的监控压力下受损。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.snap.as/Xmr1St6g.ico' data-full='https://i.snap.as/Xmr1St6g.ico' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49731356' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/live/live-fed-rate-hike-expected-inflation-worries-push-up-bond-yields-2026-09-16/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fed hikes rates as inflation worries push up bond yields</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美联储在通胀担忧仍存的背景下再次加息，推动国债收益率走高</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 11:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 182</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 249</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49731356'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Fed raised rates amid stubborn inflation, pushing bond yields higher. The move signals persistent price concerns and could raise borrowing costs for consumers and businesses, influencing tech funding and market activity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美联储在通胀担忧持续存在的背景下再次加息，推动国债收益率走高。这一举措表明价格稳定的压力仍在，可能提高个人与企业的借贷成本，影响科技领域的融资环境与市场运作。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://reclaimthenet.org/spain-blocks-archive-today-and-mirrors' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Spain Orders Blocks on Archive.today and Its Mirrors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西班牙下令封锁 Archive.today 及其镜像站点</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 23:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49772961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 182</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49772961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 193</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49772961'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Spain has ordered blocks on Archive.today and its mirrors, impacting access to archived web content. The move raises questions about digital preservation, freedom of information, and enforcement in a modern information landscape.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西班牙下令封锁 Archive.today 及其镜像站点，影响对已存档网页内容的访问。这一举措引发关于数字保存、信息自由与在现代信息环境中的执法等问题的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49749771' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49749771' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://linguisticdiscovery.com/posts/pre-greek/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pre-Greek: The lost language hidden within Ancient Greek</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>先于希腊语的失落语言藏在古希腊之中</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 20:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49749771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49749771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49749771'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linguists reconstruct a pre-Greek substrate language that contributed to the development of Ancient Greek, shedding light on how languages blend and influence each other. The discovery helps map language contact in the ancient Mediterranean and informs theories of language change.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究揭示了古希腊语之前的失落底质语言，及其在古希腊中的影响，帮助勾勒语言接触、演变的历史图景。此发现有助于理解语言如何在地中海区域相互影响与变迁理论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/c7/75/c775a216-7ca2-4c5f-96a7-f2defe0fd756/content/images/size/w1200/2026/09/map--3840x2160---1-.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/c7/75/c775a216-7ca2-4c5f-96a7-f2defe0fd756/content/images/size/w1200/2026/09/map--3840x2160---1-.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49717605' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/GraafHenk/numberwang' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WangNet – 1.8 MB, zero-dependency Numberwang adjudication in 11 languages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WangNet——1.8 MB、零依赖的 Numberwang 判定，覆盖 11 种语言</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 12:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49717605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49717605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49717605'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WangNet is a compact, 1.8 MB, zero-dependency model for Numberwang adjudication available in 11 languages. The project demonstrates how tiny, self-contained ML tooling can perform lightweight linguistic tasks offline, without external dependencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WangNet 是一个 1.8MB、零依赖的 Numberwang 判定模型，支持 11 种语言。该项目展示了体积极小、可离线运行的机器学习工具在语言任务上的可用性，以及对依赖的剥离所带来的便携性好处。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/efd248f6318224bcea125f47039b7d601940d6cbe133884b10238f1aae3f8a33/GraafHenk/numberwang' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/efd248f6318224bcea125f47039b7d601940d6cbe133884b10238f1aae3f8a33/GraafHenk/numberwang' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepMind 研究院阐述了其使命、研究领域与协作模式，定位为AI科学的核心枢纽。页面强调跨学科合作以及对长期安全与对齐目标的关注。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49768921' data-tags='AI,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49768921' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/tech-industry/artificial-intelligence/microsoft-director-called-ai-scraping-the-largest-theft-of-labor-in-human-history-while-openai-head-brands-chatgpt-an-existential-threat-to-publishers-revelations-come-from-legal-briefs-filed-in-nyt-lawsuit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft director: AI scraping &#x27;the largest theft of labor in human history&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软高管：AI 抓取数据是“人类历史上最大的劳动盗窃”</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 11:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49768921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 176</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49768921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49768921'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A legal brief quotes a Microsoft director claiming that AI scraping training data amounts to the largest theft of labor in history, underscoring publisher concerns and legal pressure over data rights and model training.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法庭文件中的一位微软高管将 AI 抓取训练数据称为“历史上最大的劳动盗窃”，凸显出版商对数据权利的担忧以及针对训练数据的法律压力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/tfUHUik9dBB9GKZAFqC6u4-2000-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/tfUHUik9dBB9GKZAFqC6u4-2000-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49742353' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49742353' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://about.gitlab.com/blog/rate-limit-change-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rate limits on GitLab.com are changing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitLab.com 的速率限制将调整</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 08:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49742353' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 172</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49742353' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49742353'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitLab.com is changing its rate limit policy. The update could adjust per-user quotas and burst allowances. The change will affect API-heavy workflows, CI pipelines, and automation, so teams should prepare by auditing usage and adjusting clients.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitLab.com 将调整速率限制策略，可能改变每个账户的配额与突发访问权限。此变动将影响 API 密集型工作流、CI/CD 流水线与自动化脚本，因此团队应通过审查使用情况并相应调整客户端来做好准备。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://res.cloudinary.com/about-gitlab-com/image/upload/v1785336708/ww4mlighwlvvzgnmbe6w.png' data-full='https://res.cloudinary.com/about-gitlab-com/image/upload/v1785336708/ww4mlighwlvvzgnmbe6w.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49731054' data-tags='Programming,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49731054' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opensource.googleblog.com/2022/06/Vectorized%20and%20performance%20portable%20Quicksort.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vectorized and performance-portable Quicksort (2022)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>向量化与性能可移植的快速排序（2022）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 11:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49731054'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google Open Source describes a vectorized Quicksort that stays fast across architectures thanks to portable SIMD. The approach reduces maintenance costs for library developers and speeds up data-heavy workloads on diverse hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 开源团队介绍了一种向量化、跨体系结构可移植的快速排序实现，利用通用 SIMD 提升跨平台的执行效率。此做法降低了库开发的维护成本，并提升了在多样硬件上的大规模数据处理速度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49776717' data-tags='Education,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49776717' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gadgetreview.com/singapore-is-paying-people-to-put-down-their-phones-and-read-books' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Singapore’s National Library Board offers micropayments to build reading habits</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新加坡国家图书馆局推出微支付以培养阅读习惯</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 20, 2026 / 08:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49776717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49776717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49776717'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Singapore&#x27;s National Library Board is piloting micropayments to boost reading and reduce smartphone time. The program rewards reading activities with small payments, using behavioral prompts to build lasting reading habits; it also raises questions about cost, privacy, and scalability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新加坡国家图书馆局推出微支付计划，旨在通过小额奖励培养阅读习惯并减少成瘾性手机使用。该计划以阅读活动为激励对象，结合行为经济学原理，但也引发成本、隐私与可扩展性方面的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gadgetreview.com/wp-content/uploads/Singapore-Is-Paying-People-to-Put-Down-Their-Phones-and-Read-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://www.gadgetreview.com/wp-content/uploads/Singapore-Is-Paying-People-to-Put-Down-Their-Phones-and-Read-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49685835' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49685835' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.damtp.cam.ac.uk/events/strings02/dirac/speach.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Relation Between Mathematics and Physics by Paul Dirac (1939)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>狄拉克：数学与物理的关系</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 09:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49685835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49685835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49685835'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dirac argued for a deep and practical link between math and physics, where mathematical structures guide physical insight and aesthetic beauty matters for theory choice. The talk helps frame how foundational math influences modern theoretical physics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>狄拉克阐述数学与物理之间的深层联系，认为数学结构为物理直觉提供指引，理论选择也受美学影响。此文有助于理解现代理论物理的基础性数学作用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49746406' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49746406' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sandordargo.com/blog/2026/09/16/cpp26-trivial-infinite-loops' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>C++26: Trivial infinite loops are no longer undefined behaviour</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C++26：琐碎的无限循环不再是未定义行为</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 13:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49746406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49746406' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 266</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49746406'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>C++26 clarifies that trivial infinite loops are not undefined behavior, closing a long-standing corner case. This reduces surprising compiler- or optimizer-induced diagnostics when code contains simple loops, improving portability and predictability for low-level systems code. Developers can rely on more consistent semantics in loop-heavy code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>C++26 将简单无限循环不再视为未定义行为，结束长期的边界问题。这将减少编译器或优化器在简单循环中的意外诊断，提升低级系统代码的可移植性与可预期性。开发者在涉及大量循环的代码中将获得更一致的语义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sandordargo.com/assets/img/SANDOR_DARGO_ROUND.JPG' data-full='https://www.sandordargo.com/assets/img/SANDOR_DARGO_ROUND.JPG' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49697171' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/h-1b-visa-us-government' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New $100k H-1B visa fee pushes tech jobs offshore</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新高额 H-1B 签证费推动科技岗位外流</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 14, 2026 / 07:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49697171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 166</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49697171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 363</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49697171'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U.S. has introduced a new $100k filing fee for H-1B petitions, lifting the cost of bringing foreign tech talent. The higher price could nudge employers toward offshore hiring or automation, with implications for the domestic tech workforce and innovation pipeline.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国对 H-1B 签证申请新增 10 万美元费用，显著提高雇佣外国技术人员的成本。更高的成本可能促使雇主将工作外包到海外或加速自动化，影响本土科技产业和创新链条。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67709071&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C125%2C0%2C125' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67709071&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C125%2C0%2C125' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49776627' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49776627' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://toolguyd.com/sherline-tools-shutting-down-usa-production/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sherline Tools Is Going Out of Business</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Sherline Tools 将停止经营</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 20, 2026 / 08:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49776627' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49776627' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 112</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49776627'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sherline Tools announced it is going out of business, ending USA production of its precision hobby tools. The closure reshapes the tooling ecosystem for hobbyists and small workshops, potentially pushing buyers toward overseas suppliers or alternatives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Sherline Tools 宣布将停止经营，USA 产线告停，精密工量工具的爱好者与小型工作室的供应格局将被重新洗牌，市场可能转向海外供应商或其他替代品。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/toolguyd.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Sherline-Precision-Tools-for-Precision-Work-with-Lathe.jpg?resize=600%2C495&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/toolguyd.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Sherline-Precision-Tools-for-Precision-Work-with-Lathe.jpg?resize=600%2C495&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49712746' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49712746' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/asia-pacific/bond-selloff-drives-us-benchmark-beyond-5-stocks-rattled-2026-09-15/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Global bond yields hit 2008 highs, raising stakes for big borrowers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球债券收益率攀升至2008年高位，令大型借款人承受更大压力</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 07:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49712746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49712746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 172</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49712746'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Global bond yields surged to levels unseen since 2008, raising borrowing costs for governments and corporations with large debt loads. The sell-off increases refinancing risk and can dampen investment as rates stay elevated. The move underscores macro headwinds for borrowers and the sensitivity of markets to higher rates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球债券收益率升至2008年以来的高位，推高政府和高负债企业的借款成本。高利率环境放大再融资风险，可能抑制投资与扩张。此举凸显借款人面临的宏观逆风，以及市场对利率上升的敏感性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49746290' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49746290' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://flet.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flet 1.0 – Build cross-platform apps in Python</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flet 1.0：用 Python 构建跨平台应用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 13:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49746290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49746290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49746290'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flet 1.0 lets developers build cross-platform GUI apps in Python with a lightweight, web-based rendering model, bypassing heavy toolchains. It lowers the barrier to desktop UIs and could pressure established stacks by enabling rapid iteration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Flet 1.0 允许开发者用 Python 构建跨平台 GUI 应用，采用轻量的网页渲染模型，省去繁重的工具链。它降低了桌面应用门槛，或通过更快的迭代速度对现有技术栈形成压力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://flet.dev/img/flet-logo.svg' data-full='https://flet.dev/img/flet-logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49730239' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49730239' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(201) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://temertymedicine.utoronto.ca/news/emergency-room-visits-gambling-disorders-nearly-doubled-after-expanded-online-gambling-market' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ER visits for gambling disorders doubled after expanded online gambling market</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在线博彩扩张后赌博障碍就诊激增</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 10:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49730239' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49730239' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 157</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49730239'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study links expanded online gambling markets to a near doubling of emergency room visits for gambling disorders. The findings underscore public health concerns and the need for targeted prevention and support services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示，在线博彩市场扩张后赌博障碍相关的急诊就诊人数接近翻倍，暴露出公共卫生隐患，并强调需强化预防与支援服务。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://temertymedicine.utoronto.ca/sites/default/files/styles/social_image/public/assets/images/Forrest%20and%20Myran_1140x760.jpg?itok=cJ61qU3I' data-full='https://temertymedicine.utoronto.ca/sites/default/files/styles/social_image/public/assets/images/Forrest%20and%20Myran_1140x760.jpg?itok=cJ61qU3I' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49731282' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49731282' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/vinnylarouge/jevlike' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reverse-engineered Jev-like model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>反向工程的 Jev 风格模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 11:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731282' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49731282'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The reverse-engineered Jev-like model reveals how similar architectures operate and behave under testing. It raises questions about licensing, safety, and the ease of reproducing proprietary capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Jev 风格模型的反向工程展示了类似架构在测试中的运行与行为，也引发关于许可、安全性以及复现性等方面的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 14:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49770256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49770256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49770256'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shows how an integer overflow can defeat the Dream Devourer boss in Chrono Trigger, illustrating classic retro-game bugs and the fragility of certain game logic.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>展示在 Chrono Trigger 中通过整型溢出击败 Dream Devourer 的方法，体现了早期游戏中的经典漏洞与某些游戏逻辑的脆弱性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49768833' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49768833' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(204) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bartosz.fenski.pl/modern-fs-benchmark/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Btrfs/ZFS/bcachefs under workloads classic benchmarks skip</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>经典基准下的 Btrfs/ZFS/bcachefs 现代工作负载对比（跳过部分基准）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 11:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49768833' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49768833' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49768833'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A modern filesystem benchmark pits Btrfs, ZFS, and bcachefs against contemporary workloads, and notes that some classic benchmarks are skipped. The results illustrate tradeoffs between data integrity, performance, and maturity across the three FS options, with ZFS offering mature features and bcachefs showing potential but less maturity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这项现代文件系统基准测试在 Btrfs、ZFS 与 bcachefs 之间进行比较，指出某些经典基准被跳过。结果揭示数据完整性、性能与成熟度之间的取舍，ZFS 功能更成熟，bcachefs 仍具潜力但不如 ZFS 成熟。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://poltora.dev/redis' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Redis City – Explore how Redis works in an interactive 3D model</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 12, 2026 / 12:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49676425' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49676425' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Redis City is an interactive 3D model that reveals how Redis works, from data structures to memory layout and event-driven execution. It serves as a hands-on learning tool for developers curious about in-memory databases and performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Redis City 提供一个互动的三维模型，展示 Redis 的内部工作原理、数据结构、内存布局与执行模型。对想要透彻理解内存数据库性能的开发者是一个直观的学习工具。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Infinite-Parameter LLMs: Generating and Adapting Weights from Live Data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无限参数 LLM：从实时数据生成与自适应权重</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 09:55 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49743483'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes LLMs with theoretically unbounded parameters that can generate and adjust weights from live data, enabling continual learning without retraining from scratch. The concept explores how models could stay up-to-date with streaming inputs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出无限参数的 LLM 的设想，能够从实时数据生成并自适应权重，以实现持续学习。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49684356' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Speedstu/CUDA-for-AMD-Windows' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CUDA for AMD on Windows</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 上的 AMD CUDA 实现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 07:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49684356'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A community project brings CUDA-like tooling to AMD GPUs on Windows, enabling CUDA-dependent workflows to run on non-NVIDIA hardware. While not a drop-in replacement, it broadens hardware choices for ML developers and hobbyists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>社区项目让 AMD 显卡在 Windows 上获得类似 CUDA 的体验，扩展了不能使用 NVIDIA 的硬件场景。尽管不是完全替代，但为 ML 开发者和爱好者带来更广的硬件选择。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49692844' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49692844' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://evanhahn.com/posts/2026-09-13-programmers-dislike-reduce/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anecdotally, programmers dislike &quot;reduce&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>据说 程序员不爱用 reduce</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 23:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49692844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49692844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49692844'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Many programmers avoid reduce due to readability, potential for subtle bugs with empty inputs, and perf concerns; the post suggests simpler looping or explicit alternatives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据说 程序员不喜欢 reduce，因为可读性差、处理空序列时容易出错、以及潜在的性能问题；文章建议使用更直观的循环或显式替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://evanhahn.com/images/logo_white.png' data-full='https://evanhahn.com/images/logo_white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49690260' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49690260' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(214) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.interconnects.ai/p/open-source-ai-reading-list' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open-source AI and open models reading list</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开源 AI 与开源模型阅读清单</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一份关于开源 AI 项目与模型的精选阅读清单，展示了相对于专有系统的透明、社区驱动替代方案的全貌。内容涵盖许可、安全、治理以及实际应用场景。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49703003' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://entelligence.ai/blogs/gpt-5.6-luna-vs-gpt-6-astra-is-a-1.20-model-good-enough-for-code-review' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.6 Luna vs. GPT-6 Astra: Is a $1.20 Model Good Enough for Code Review?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.6 Luna 与 GPT-6 Astra：1.20美元模型是否足以进行代码审查？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 14, 2026 / 12:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49703003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49703003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49703003'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An informal comparison tests a very cheap model against a more capable one on code-review tasks. The cheaper model can surface some issues and offer suggestions, but it misses nuanced reasoning and project-wide context. The results illustrate cost-performance tradeoffs when choosing AI copilots for software review.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>非正式的对比测试在代码审查任务上比较了一个极低成本的模型与一个更强的模型。更便宜的模型能发现部分问题并给出建议，但在细致推理和整项目上下文方面存在不足。结果凸显了在选择代码审查AI助手时的性价比权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://framerusercontent.com/images/gPrTReKBwM5bVRyIqVcgBE4kEAk.png' data-full='https://framerusercontent.com/images/gPrTReKBwM5bVRyIqVcgBE4kEAk.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arkeonews.net/4400-year-old-tomb-of-an-egyptian-judge-found-at-saqqara-with-colors-still-on-the-walls/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>4,400-Year-Old Tomb of Egyptian Judge Found at Saqqara with Colors on Walls</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>萨卡拉发现的4400年埃及法官墓，墙壁仍有色彩</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 12, 2026 / 11:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49675817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 154</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49675817' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49675817'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discovered at Saqqara, the 4400-year-old tomb of an Egyptian judge preserves wall colors, offering rare insight into burial art and practices. The find highlights how new discoveries can reshape understandings of ancient law and society.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在萨卡拉发现的一座4400年前埃及法官的墓葬，墙面仍保留色彩，为研究当时的葬俗与艺术提供珍贵线索。此发现有助于重新理解古埃及的司法与社会结构。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49694035' data-tags='AI,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49694035' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Intelligence per Watt: Measuring Intelligence Efficiency of Local AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每瓦特智能：衡量本地 AI 的智能效率</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 14, 2026 / 02:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49694035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49694035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49694035'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An arXiv paper proposes a metric called Intelligence per Watt to measure how much useful intelligence a local AI model delivers per watt of energy. The framework benchmarks energy efficiency for edge deployments and discusses implications for deploying AI offline.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇学术论文提出“每瓦特智能”作为衡量本地 AI 智能产出与能耗之比的度量。该框架用于评估边缘部署的能效，并讨论在离线环境中部署 AI 的影响与取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49713024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 150</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49713024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49713024'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI inference hardware is undergoing a fundamental shift in 2026, with new chips, memory architectures, and accelerators driving cheaper, faster model deployment. The piece surveys how this hardware evolution affects cost, latency, and real-world accessibility of large models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2026 年 AI 推理硬件正发生根本性转变，新的芯片、内存架构与加速器推动模型部署更便宜、速度更快。文章梳理了这一硬件演进如何影响成本、延迟以及大模型的实际可用性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 Verus 开发可证明正确的 Rust 代码</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 14, 2026 / 09:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49700153' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49700153' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49700153'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Verus enables formal verification of Rust code, helping prove correctness properties beyond tests. The post explains how Verus integrates with Rust and why this matters for systems where provable guarantees reduce risk, while noting current limitations and typical workflow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Verus 为 Rust 代码提供可证明正确性的途径，帮助对关键系统的正确性属性进行形式化验证。文章介绍它如何与 Rust 集成，以及在降低风险方面的潜在意义，同时也提到当前的局限与实际工作流程。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49713262' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(223) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://josprague.com/blog/the-css-zen-garden-dream-finally-shipped/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The CSS Zen Garden dream, finally shipped</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CSS Zen Garden 梦想终于实现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 07:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49713262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49713262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49713262'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The CSS Zen Garden ethos—separating content from presentation—gets tangible form, letting designers radically style pages with CSS alone. This shipped project demonstrates the enduring power of CSS theming and the importance of clean HTML structure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CSS Zen Garden 的理念——将内容与呈现分离——如今有了切实的实现，让设计师仅用 CSS 就能对页面进行极致美化。这个实现凸显了 CSS 主题化的持久力量与干净 HTML 结构的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://josprague.com/og.png' data-full='https://josprague.com/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49756671' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(224) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/browserbase/stagehand' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We made Playwright 2x faster and 80% more token efficient</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对话式大语言模型通过利用线索与模式匹配来营造“洞察力”，类似灵媒欺骗机制，引发对信任、透明度与用户体验的担忧。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49730069' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49730069' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tigrisdata.com/blog/objgit-packfiles/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You can run Git on object storage if you re-make packfiles</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过重新打包 packfiles，可以让 Git 在对象存储上运行</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 10:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49730069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 139</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49730069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49730069'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Remaking packfiles enables Git to work with object storage, unlocking cloud-native workflows without relying on a POSIX filesystem. The approach trades some latency for scalability and bandwidth efficiency, suitable for centralized repos accessed over the network, but may be unfriendly for low-latency scenarios.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过重新打包 packfiles，可以让 Git 在对象存储上运行，从而实现云原生工作流。该做法在可扩展性和带宽效率上做出权衡，适合网络访问的集中仓库，但在对低延迟要求较高的场景下可能不太友好。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.tigrisdata.com/blog/assets/images/hero-image-81fc688a5cf1e95701d66a80813da3c1.webp' data-full='https://www.tigrisdata.com/blog/assets/images/hero-image-81fc688a5cf1e95701d66a80813da3c1.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49746407' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49746407' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(227) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://liamnugent.me/posts/what-you-dont-build/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The most important product decision is what you don&#x27;t build</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>最重要的产品决策是你不去做什么</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 13:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49746407' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49746407' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49746407'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that constraining scope and saying no to features is often the biggest strategic move in product work. By avoiding feature creep, teams can ship faster, maintain quality, and focus on a defensible value proposition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为收缩范围、拒绝无关功能往往是产品工作的最关键战略举措。避免功能堆砌有助于更快发布、保持质量，并专注于具有明确价值的核心定位。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://liamnugent.me/img/og_image-1.jpg' data-full='https://liamnugent.me/img/og_image-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zed.dev/blog/delta-public-beta' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Replacing Pull Requests with Delta</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Delta 取代拉取请求</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 07:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49727245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49727245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49727245'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Delta proposes replacing traditional pull requests with delta-based diffs, enabling more granular and streaming code reviews. The approach aims to speed up collaboration, improve traceability, and reduce review chatter. The public beta signals growing interest in alternative review workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Delta 提出以增量差分替代传统的拉取请求，使代码评审可以更细粒度、流式化。此做法旨在加速协作、提升可追溯性，并减少冗余评论。公开的公测阶段显示对替代性评审工作流的关注度在上升。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(235) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rust-glancer.github.io/blog/why-lsp-is-hard/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why building a Rust LSP is hard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么用 Rust 构建 LSP 很困难</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 15:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49734131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49734131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49734131'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rust LSP development is hard due to Rust&#x27;s ownership model, lifetimes, and macros complicating static analysis. It explains why robust Rust language servers remain a difficult engineering problem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rust 的所有权模型、生命周期和宏系统让实现语言服务器变得异常困难。文中讨论了增量编译、诊断准确性以及大代码库下的 IDE 集成等挑战，并解释了为何要构建稳定的 Rust 语言服务器仍然是一个棘手的工程问题。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 09:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49715318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49715318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49715318'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This post reviews and compares various malloc implementations, outlining performance trade-offs, fragmentation behavior, and suitability for different workloads. The comparison helps developers choose an allocator that fits their application&#x27;s memory patterns.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章对不同 malloc 实现进行了比较，分析了性能权衡、碎片化行为以及对不同工作负载的适用性，有助于开发者根据应用的内存特征做出选择。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mathoverflow.net/questions/515260/how-do-we-prevent-mathematics-from-devolving-into-the-medieval-era-of-secrecy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How do we prevent mathemathics from devolving into the Medieval Era of secrecy?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何防止数学沦为秘密主义的中世纪时代？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 10:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49715936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49715936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49715936'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The question asks how to preserve openness in mathematical research in the face of paywalls and closed collaboration. It advocates open access, transparent proofs, and community norms to keep mathematics collaborative and accessible.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该问题探讨如何在面对付费墙与封闭合作者的情境下，维持数学研究的开放性。主张开放获取、透明证明和健康的社区规范以保持学科的协作性与可获取性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(238) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.amazon.science/blog/why-dont-machine-learning-research-agents-overfit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why don&#x27;t machine learning research agents overfit?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么机器学习研究代理不会过拟合？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 14, 2026 / 09:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49699648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49699648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49699648'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Machine learning research agents often generalize differently from standard training pipelines, thanks to diverse objectives and broad evaluation. The piece outlines mechanisms that help prevent overfitting in automated experiment systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>与传统训练流程相比，机器学习研究代理常在更广泛的目标和评测下工作，从而降低过拟合风险。文章讨论了在自动化实验系统中有助于提升泛化的机制。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49697477' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(243) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nathannaveen.dev/posts/dropping-ebpf-cpu-cost-by-90/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dropping eBPF CPU Cost by About 90% with Memoization (Not AI Gen)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过记忆化将 eBPF CPU 成本降至约 90%（非 AI 生成）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 14, 2026 / 07:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49697477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 127</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49697477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49697477'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Memoization cuts eBPF CPU usage by roughly 90%, without relying on AI generation. The technique caches results of expensive kernel-space computations, reducing per-packet processing and boosting throughput for observability and networking workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过记忆化缓存昂贵的 eBPF 计算结果，相关实现将 CPU 开销约降低 90%，不依赖 AI 生成。此举能显著降低每个数据包的处理成本，提升观测与网络工作负载的吞吐量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nathannaveen.dev/' data-full='https://nathannaveen.dev/' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(246) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lawfaremedia.org/article/25-years-of-mass-surveillance-is-enough' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>25 Years of Mass Surveillance Is Enough [Auth: Cindy Cohn; Bruce Schneier]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>25 年的大规模监控已经够了</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 05:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49711309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49711309' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49711309'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cindy Cohn and Bruce Schneier argue that 25 years of mass surveillance is enough, calling for reforms to protect civil liberties while maintaining security. The piece frames surveillance as a decisive policy issue.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cindy Cohn 与 Bruce Schneier 认为25 年的大规模监控已足够，呼吁通过改革来保护公民自由与安全。这一议题被视为一个关键的政策抉择。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(247) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://utf-8000.jb2170.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UTF-8000: Unlimited UTF-8</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>UTF-8000：无限制的 UTF-8</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 22:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49772677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 125</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49772677' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49772677'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative exploration of a hypothetical UTF-8 variant aimed at removing practical string length limits, and the implications for software, databases, and text processing. The piece weighs memory, performance, and standardization challenges in pursuing an &#x27;unbounded&#x27; encoding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对一个假设性的 UTF-8 变体进行挑衅性探讨，试图移除文本长度的实际限制，以及这对软件、数据库和文本处理的影响。文章权衡在追求“无限制”编码时的内存、性能与标准化挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 20, 2026 / 08:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49777106' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49777106' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49777106'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A demonstration shows Laya running on Mac M4 offline via CoreML at about 45 inferences per second. This showcases strong on-device inference capabilities and the potential for latency-free AI apps that don’t need cloud connectivity.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49683033' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ras.ac.uk/news-and-press/research-highlights/fingerprints-inside-sun-could-reveal-if-it-once-swallowed-planet' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Fingerprints&#x27; inside the Sun could reveal if it once swallowed a planet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>太阳内部的指纹可能揭示它是否曾经吞噬过一颗行星</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 05:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49683033' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49683033' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49683033'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astronomers detect signatures inside the Sun that could record a past planetary ingestion, offering a novel way to probe solar interior dynamics and planetary formation history.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>天文学家在太阳内层发现“指纹”式信号，可能记录了曾经吞噬行星的历史。该发现为研究太阳内部结构和行星形成史提供新线索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ras.ac.uk/sites/default/files/2026-09/star_swallowing_a_planet.png' data-full='https://ras.ac.uk/sites/default/files/2026-09/star_swallowing_a_planet.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49763883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49763883' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 144</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49743934' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23696' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Compiler-style optimization for drawing via Skia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过编译器风格的优化提升 Skia 绘制性能</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 10:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49743934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49743934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49743934'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Presents compiler-inspired optimizations for Skia drawing routines, aiming to reduce overhead, improve vectorization, and speed up rendering across platforms.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49728733' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49728733' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.jim-nielsen.com/2026/stop-with-the-uptime-percentage/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can we stop with the uptime percentages?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>别再只盯着正常运行时间百分比</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 08:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49728733' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49728733' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49728733'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues uptime percentage alone obscures reliability issues; it advocates more meaningful metrics like latency, MTTR, saturation, and error budgets. It suggests focusing on user experience rather than blunt numbers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为仅靠正常运行时间百分比无法全面反映可靠性，应关注更有意义的指标，如延迟、修复时间、容量饱和和错误预算，强调以用户体验为导向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.jim-nielsen.com/blog/2026/uptime-claude-github.png' data-full='https://cdn.jim-nielsen.com/blog/2026/uptime-claude-github.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
