</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49714096'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TSMC disclosed details about the next-gen A14-class process node, highlighting improvements in transistor density and power efficiency. The reveal underscores ongoing leadership in advanced nodes and could impact future Apple silicon performance, while manufacturing constraints remain a consideration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>台积电披露下一代 A14 节点的关键细节，强调晶体管密度与能效的提升。这一披露显示在先进节点上的持续领跑地位，可能推动苹果新一代芯片的性能，但产能与良率仍是需要关注的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49700255' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49700255' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(263) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/automatic-key-exchange-for-origins/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare AKE cuts origin HelloRetryRequests from 52% to 3.7%</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare AKE 将原始端点的 HelloRetryRequests 从 52% 降至 3.7%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 14, 2026 / 10:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49700255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49700255' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49700255'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare’s Automatic Key Exchange reduces TLS 1.3 HelloRetryRequests dramatically, cutting handshake latency and easing origin load. The improvement matters for large-scale TLS workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 的自动密钥交换显著降低 TLS 1.3 的 HelloRetryRequests，减少握手延迟并缓解源端压力，对大规模 TLS 工作负载意义重大。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M1PVVA86NJR4N0MTKCH06A9V.01M1PVVAXCHV18R9AZA18HS4AQ.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M1PVVA86NJR4N0MTKCH06A9V.01M1PVVAXCHV18R9AZA18HS4AQ.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49721548' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.archive.org/2026/09/11/negativland-culture-jamming-and-the-art-of-making-something-new/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Negativland, Culture Jamming, and the Art of Making Something New</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Negativland、文化干扰及创造新事物的艺术</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 19:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49721548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49721548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49721548'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores Negativland&#x27;s culture jamming as a practice of recombining existing media to generate new meaning and critique. The piece touches on originality, copyright and the artist&#x27;s method of turning borrowed material into provocative work. It offers takeaways for creators about transformation, attribution, and the power—and limits—of remix.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨 Negativland 的文化干扰艺术实践，展示如何通过再混合现有素材创造新意义并批评主流媒体。讨论原创性、版权与通过改编素材激发争议的创作方法。为创作者提供关于再造、署名与混合艺术边界的启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.archive.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/img.evbuc_.png' data-full='https://blog.archive.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/img.evbuc_.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 10:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49768347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 109</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49768347' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49768347'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Part two surveys classic Mac-era symbols, icons, and glyphs that faded as macOS evolved. It highlights how UI conventions migrate and what older users remember about the Mac interface.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>第二部分回顾在 macOS 演化中逐渐淡出的经典 Mac 符号、图标与标识，揭示用户界面规范的演变及旧时界面的记忆。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://unsung.aresluna.org/_media/key-symbols-we-lost-to-time-pt-2-the-mac-side/ogimage.jpg' data-full='https://unsung.aresluna.org/_media/key-symbols-we-lost-to-time-pt-2-the-mac-side/ogimage.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49754250'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers find that AI chatbots are increasingly adept at shaping beliefs and opinions through tailored messaging and social dynamics. The findings raise concerns about manipulation, misinformation, and how to safeguard users from subtle influence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现 AI 聊天机器人在通过定制化信息与社交互动改变观点方面日益高效，引发对操控、错误信息以及如何保护用户免受潜在影响的担忧。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that smart rings are failing to deliver practical value, with limited apps, privacy concerns, and marginal benefits for most users. It reflects a broader trend away from niche wearables toward more capable devices or simpler, more private solutions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为智能戒指难以给用户带来实质性价值，应用场景有限、隐私与安全性存在隐忧，收益对大多数用户而言过于微弱。反映了从小众可穿戴设备向更有用的设备或更简洁、私密解决方案演变的趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Learning another language may keep your brain healthy as you age, with research showing cognitive benefits from bilingualism. The piece summarizes how language learning strengthens executive function and neural reserve, with practical tips to start.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(278) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thenextweb.com/news/hugging-face-delangue-openai-100m-compute-traces-demand' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hugging Face is billing OpenAI $100M for hacking it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hugging Face 向 OpenAI 收取 1 亿美元的计算资源费</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 10:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49716241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49716241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49716241'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hugging Face reportedly bills OpenAI $100M for compute traces or usage, signaling a recent financial dispute over how open-source tooling interacts with closed AI labs. The controversy spotlights licensing, attribution, and monetization frictions in AI ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称 Hugging Face 正向 OpenAI 收取 1 亿美元的计算资源费，凸显开源工具与封闭式 AI 实验室之间在计费和使用追踪上的分歧。事件暴露了 AI 生态系统内的许可、署名与盈利模型的摩擦。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.thenextweb.com/2026/07/Hugging-Face-breach.jpg' data-full='https://media.thenextweb.com/2026/07/Hugging-Face-breach.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49742437' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49742437' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(279) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://minimallysufficient.com/posts/llm-classification-is-feature-extraction/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLM Classification Is Feature Engineering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LLM 分类就是特征工程</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 08:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49742437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49742437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49742437'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that LLM-based classification is essentially feature extraction, reframing why we should treat labels as derived from representations. It encourages practitioners to rethink how they build and test classifiers with LLMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论点：LLM 分类实质上是特征提取，重新定义了标签、表示和评估的关系。该文讨论对鲁棒性、数据管线和工具链的影响，并呼吁重新思考在 LLM 上构建分类器的做法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://minimallysufficient.com/posts/llm-classification-is-feature-extraction/assets/coefficients.png' data-full='https://minimallysufficient.com/posts/llm-classification-is-feature-extraction/assets/coefficients.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Uber outlines engineering practices to prevent retry storms from cascading across systems, including idempotency, backoff strategies, and queueing controls.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(282) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://crocidb.com/post/when-the-fractional-part-of-a-float-fixes-your-shader/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When the fractional part of a float fixes your shader</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>浮点数的小数部分如何修正着色器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 06:20 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49726614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49726614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49726614'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Using the fractional part of a float can fix shader artifacts by avoiding precision pitfalls in UV tiling and texture sampling. The author demonstrates practical cases and explains why fract() helps keep visuals clean in real-time rendering. A small math trick can unlock noticeable rendering improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在着色器中利用浮点数的小数部分来避免精度问题，可以修正伪影，尤其是在 UV 坐标和纹理取样时。文中给出实际案例并解释了 fract() 为何有助于保持实时渲染中的视觉清晰。这个小小的数学技巧就能带来显著的渲染改进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://crocidb.com/post/when-the-fractional-part-of-a-float-fixes-your-shader/images/Pastedimage20260907235534.png' data-full='https://crocidb.com/post/when-the-fractional-part-of-a-float-fixes-your-shader/images/Pastedimage20260907235534.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49780064'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open source funding remains brittle because users rarely pay for OSS directly. The piece argues for forcing payments or creating platform-level incentives to sustain OSS, weighing potential benefits against freedom and innovation costs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开源资金持续性不足，文章主张通过强制付费或在平台层面引入激励来支持开源项目的资金来源。讨论了不同筹资方案的利弊，以及在保持自由与创新的同时确保可持续性的挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(286) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/black-holes-or-black-hole-stars-astronomers-spar-over-webb-telescopes-little-red-dots-20260914/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Black Holes or Black Hole Stars? Astronomers Spar over &#x27;Little Red Dots&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑洞还是黑洞恒星？天文学家就‘小红点’争论</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 08:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49756121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49756121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49756121'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Webb telescope data fueling a debate: are the &#x27;Little Red Dots&#x27; evidence of black holes or stars with black-hole-like properties? The article outlines the competing interpretations, the observations behind them, and why classification matters for galaxy evolution and accretion physics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于詹姆斯韦伯望远镜观测到的‘小红点’，学界就它是否来自黑洞还是带有黑洞特征的恒星展开辩论。文章梳理了证据与不同解释，以及这一分类对理解黑洞形成和星系演化的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Black-Hole-Star-cr-Mark-Belan-Social-1.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Black-Hole-Star-cr-Mark-Belan-Social-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49731996' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(287) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://daily.jstor.org/how-the-railways-killed-a-medieval-law/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deodands put a price on objects that caused death</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>致死物（Deodands）在中世纪法律中的定价制度</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 12:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731996' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731996' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49731996'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains deodands—objects that caused a death—and how medieval courts assigned liability and value to them. The practice reveals evolving notions of responsibility, property, and the moral economy around accidents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解释“致死物”（Deodands）这一概念及中世纪法院如何对其定责与估价。该做法揭示了当时对事故责任、财产与道德经济的演变认知。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://daily.jstor.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/how_the_railways_killed_a_medieval_law_1050x700.jpg' data-full='https://daily.jstor.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/how_the_railways_killed_a_medieval_law_1050x700.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49754687' data-tags='Legal,Privacy,Security,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49754687' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(288) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://knightcolumbia.org/content/second-circuit-allows-government-to-search-electronic-devices-at-the-border-without-any-suspicion' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Second Circuit allows government to search electronic devices at the border</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>第二巡回法院允许在边境搜索电子设备</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 07:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49754687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49754687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49754687'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Second Circuit ruled that border authorities may search electronic devices at the border without suspicion, broadening surveillance capabilities. The ruling raises civil liberties concerns and questions about oversight, data privacy, and the balance between security and Fourth Amendment protections for travelers. It has implications for developers building travel-related apps and data handling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>第二巡回法院裁定边境执法机构可在边境对电子设备进行无怀疑的搜索，扩大了监控能力。这一判决引发公民自由与数据隐私的担忧，以及对安全与第四条修正案保护之间平衡的质疑。对从事旅行相关应用与数据处理的开发者也可能产生影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://knightcolumbia.org/images/social/KFAI-social-card-updated.jpg' data-full='http://knightcolumbia.org/images/social/KFAI-social-card-updated.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49653311' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49653311' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(289) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aprilnea.me/en/blog/reverse-engineering-claude-code-antspace' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reverse-Engineering Claude Web&#x27;s MicroVM: Uncovering Anthropic&#x27;s Hidden Antspace</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>逆向工程 Claude Web 的 MicroVM：揭示 Anthropic 的隐藏 Antspace</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 10, 2026 / 20:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49653311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 101</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49653311' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49653311'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers reverse-engineer Claude Web&#x27;s MicroVM and uncover a hidden Antspace component, shedding light on how Anthropic isolates AI workloads. The findings raise questions about security, reproducibility, and the deployment of cloud-based AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员对 Claude Web 的 MicroVM 进行逆向分析，发现隐藏的 Antspace 组件，揭示 Anthropic 如何对 AI 工作负载进行隔离。结果引发关于安全性、可重复性及云端 AI 部署的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.aprilnea.me/blog/t2025/reverse-engineering-claude-code-antspace/hero.svg' data-full='https://media.aprilnea.me/blog/t2025/reverse-engineering-claude-code-antspace/hero.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49770847' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49770847' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(290) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://slop-sense.labtoagi.com/games/is-this-image-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can you tell which images are AI-generated?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你能分辨哪些图片是 AI 生成的吗？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 16:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49770847' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 100</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49770847' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49770847'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An interactive game/tests gauge whether people can reliably spot AI-generated images, highlighting current detector limitations and the ongoing arms race between real and synthetic imagery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款互动游戏/测试评估人们是否能 reliably 识别 AI 生成的图像，凸显当前检测工具的局限性以及现实与合成图像之间的竞争。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(293) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The k-server conjecture is true</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>k-server 猜想成立</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 00:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49709129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 98</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49709129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49709129'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A landmark result proving the k-server conjecture, resolving a long-standing question in online algorithms and competitive analysis. The finding clarifies the best possible competitive ratio and informs caching and scheduling theory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关键结果证明了 k-server 猜想，解决了在线算法与竞争分析中的长期难题。该定理确立了在一般度量空间上的最佳竞争比，为缓存和调度理论提供重要基石。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 13:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49688391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49688391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49688391'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2003 incident shows how flawed routing can flood critical time services, stressing the need for SNTP hardening and clean routing practices. The piece traces the misconfigurations and their impact on infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2003年的事件揭示了配置有误的路由器如何对关键的时间服务造成洪泛，强调 SNTP 服务器的防护和路由整洁的重要性。文中梳理了多处错误配置与对现代基础设施的教训。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(299) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://truthout.org/articles/warren-introduces-bill-to-ban-private-equity-from-owning-medical-practices/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bill to Ban Private Equity from Owning Medical Practices</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>禁止私募股权拥有医疗机构的法案</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 20, 2026 / 15:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49780630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49780630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49780630'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduced by Senator Warren, the bill would bar private-equity firms from owning medical practices, citing concerns about cost, access, and care quality. The move could reshape ownership structures in healthcare and influence investment dynamics in the sector.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>沃伦提出的法案旨在禁止私募股权控股医生诊所，以应对成本上升、就医可及性下降等担忧。若通过，可能改变医疗市场的所有权结构并影响投资者行为。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49649164' data-tags='Politics,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49649164' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(301) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.katjahoyer.uk/p/the-gdr-and-vietnam-from-fake-coffee' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The GDR and Vietnam: From Fake Coffee to Coffee Empire</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德意志民主共和国与越南：从假咖啡到咖啡帝国</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 10, 2026 / 12:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49649164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 93</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49649164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49649164'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece traces how East Germany manipulated coffee trade with Vietnam, including fake shipments to meet quotas and extend influence, shaping Vietnam’s coffee sector amid Cold War dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章梳理东德在越南贸易中的“假咖啡”操控，借此达到配额与影响力扩张的目的，最终影响越南的咖啡产业，揭示商品政治与冷战格局的交织。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sci.news/paleontology/spanish-diplodocus-15064.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Diplodocus, Long Thought Exclusively American, Turns Up in Spain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>被长期认为只出现在美国的梁龙在西班牙现身</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 03:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49710447' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49710447' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49710447'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Diplodocus fossil found in Spain challenges the notion that the species was exclusive to North America, expanding our view of Jurassic dinosaur distribution. The find could reshape paleogeography and guide European digs going forward.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在西班牙发现的梁龙化石挑战了其仅分布于北美的说法，拓展了对侏罗纪恐龙分布的认知。此发现可能改变古地理学研究方向，并为欧洲未来的挖掘提供线索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sci.news/images/2026/09/image_15064f-Spanish-Diplodocus.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.sci.news/images/2026/09/image_15064f-Spanish-Diplodocus.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>绳索、麻绳与线：石器时代的隐形科技</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 11, 2026 / 10:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49662246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49662246'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stone Age rope and fiber technologies enabled a range of practical innovations, from binding and lashings to nets and weave-based clothing. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>石器时代的绳索与纤维技术催生了一系列实用创新，如绑缚、网具、织物等。这些看不见的日常技术推动了在金属工具出现之前就已形成的大规模协作与贸易。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49754055' data-tags='Science,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49754055' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(312) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pubs.rsc.org/ee/article/19/13/4199/1260994/Direct-electrode-to-electrode-regeneration-of-end' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Regeneration of used batteries via electrode–electrolyte interphase dissolution</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>废旧电池再生：通过电极–电解质界面溶解实现</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 06:23 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49754055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49754055' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49754055'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate a method to regenerate spent batteries by dissolving the electrode–electrolyte interphase, enabling recovery of active materials and potentially reducing recycling costs. The approach could enhance sustainability for large-scale energy storage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究提出通过溶解电极–电解质界面来再生废旧电池的方法，便于回收活性材料并可能降低回收成本。这一思路若成熟，将提升大规模储能的可持续性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾作者对苏珊娜·西亚尼的 Buchla 打线手册的介绍，该指南聚焦打线方法，塑造了她在 Buchla 模块化系统中的标志性音色。文中强调通过直观的打线与模块化结构能够产生独特的音色，这种思路至今仍影响经典与现代合成。要点在于这位传奇艺术家的实操经验对音乐人和器材设计者仍具参考价值。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数学家打造久违的 Graph Sandwich</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 07:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49755095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 78</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49755095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49755095'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mathematicians announce a construction addressing the long-standing graph sandwich problem, a concept in extremal graph theory. The achievement advances understanding in combinatorics and provides concrete progress on a challenging open question. The work illustrates how explicit constructions can illuminate abstract theory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数学家宣布解决久等的 Graph Sandwich 问题，属于极值图理论的研究范畴。这一进展推动组合学的理解，并为一个长期未解的问题提供了具体进展，展示了显式构造在抽象理论中的作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/articles/c8jdp38z4jgo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Douglas Adams and the exterminated Doctor Who adventure</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>道格拉斯·亚当斯与被抹去的《神秘博士》冒险</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 14:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49689062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49689062' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49689062'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Douglas Adams penned a Doctor Who adventure that was planned but ultimately cancelled, and parts were erased from BBC archives. The piece recounts the backstory, why it was scrapped, and how fans and scholars have since pieced together fragments and reconstructions. It illustrates the fragility of TV history and the value of archival recovery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>道格拉斯·亚当斯曾创作并计划拍摄的《神秘博士》冒险剧本最终被取消，部分内容也从 BBC 档案中抹去。文章回顾了创作背景、取消原因，以及粉丝与学者如何拼凑碎片、尝试重构。这彰显了电视历史的脆弱性以及档案修复的重要价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_news/1200/cpsprodpb/6d0c/live/3e4afb80-ac3d-11f1-aa42-a9647fd0ba61.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/ace/branded_news/1200/cpsprodpb/6d0c/live/3e4afb80-ac3d-11f1-aa42-a9647fd0ba61.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(342) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/1802.07228' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Malicious Use of Artificial Intelligence</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工智能的恶意使用</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 18:22 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49690678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49690678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49690678'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This foundational risk assessment maps how AI could be misused across domains—from deception and misinformation to cybersecurity and privacy breaches—and outlines practical mitigation strategies. It emphasizes governance, red-teaming, and aligned incentives to reduce misuse risk as AI capabilities grow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份基础性风险评估勾勒了人工智能在各领域的潜在滥用场景，从欺骗与错误信息扩散到网络攻击与隐私侵犯，并提出切实的缓解策略，强调治理、对抗性测试和激励对齐以降低风险。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49731413' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49731413' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(343) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.ndepend.com/understanding-cyclomatic-complexity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cyclomatic Complexity in C#</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C# 的圈复杂度及其衡量</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 12:01 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49731413'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discussion of cyclomatic complexity in C# explains how this metric tracks the number of linearly independent paths through code, and why it matters for maintainability and testing. The post likely offers practical guidelines for controlling complexity through refactoring, modular design, and better testing strategies. It also notes tradeoffs between readability and performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文解读了 C# 的圈复杂度及其衡量方法，解释该指标如何反映代码中独立执行路径的数量，以及为何对可维护性和测试具有决定性影响。文章给出通过重构、模块化设计和更有效的测试策略来控制复杂度的实用建议，并讨论可读性与性能之间的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.ndepend.com/wp-content/uploads/Cyclomatic-Complexity-in-C.png' data-full='https://blog.ndepend.com/wp-content/uploads/Cyclomatic-Complexity-in-C.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49738766' data-tags='Data,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49738766' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(344) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://manticoresearch.com/blog/auto-chunking/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Better Vector Search for Long Documents: Chunking Inside Manticore Search</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 Manticore Search 内部进行分块以提升长文档向量搜索</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 03:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49738766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 73</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49738766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49738766'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Manticore Search introduces internal chunking to improve vector search for long documents. Chunking enables more accurate retrieval and scalable indexing, helping enterprise search use cases handle long texts efficiently.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Manticore Search 引入内部分块，以提升长文档的向量检索效果。分块让检索更精准、索引更可扩展，适合需要高效处理长文本的企业场景。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TailTalk 是一个用 Rust 和 Tokio 构建的现代异步用户态 AppleTalk 协议栈，演示如何用高安全性语言实现旧有网络协议的现代化实现。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49731360' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(346) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Accurate Models of AMD Matrix Cores</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AMD Matrix Core 的精确模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 11:56 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731360' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49731360' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49731360'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper builds accurate models of AMD&#x27;s Matrix Cores to predict performance and behavior, enabling better compiler optimizations and resource planning. It provides benchmarks and modeling techniques to approximate microarchitectural behavior for workloads like AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该工作给出对AMD Matrix Core的高精度建模，帮助预测性能和行为，并为编译优化和资源调度提供依据。通过一系列基准和建模方法，揭示了矩阵核心在常见工作负载中的表现特征。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(347) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/stoix-dev/sefaz-webservices-postman' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every invoice in Brazil&#x27;s economy runs on SOAP 1.2</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 12, 2026 / 20:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49679653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 71</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49679653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49679653'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Every invoice in Brazil&#x27;s economy runs on SOAP 1.2-based web services, highlighting legacy constraints that complicate modernization efforts. The post underscores the tension between stable, long-running government integrations and the push toward modern API design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>巴西的发票系统广泛依赖基于 SOAP 1.2 的 Web 服务，暴露出向现代接口迁移时的遗留约束。文章强调稳定的政府集成与向现代 API 设计之间的矛盾。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49758689' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(348) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Implications of Linguistic Illegibility for LLM Security</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LLM 安全性的语言不可读性影响</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 12:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49758689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49758689' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49758689'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers explore how linguistic illegibility—prompts or outputs that are hard for humans to read—affects LLM security. The findings highlight new angles for defense against prompt injection and data leakage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究探讨了语言不可读性对 LLM 安全性的影响，提示在对抗提示注入和信息泄露方面需要新策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(349) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://david.alvarezrosa.com/posts/optimizing-a-spin-lock/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Optimizing a Spin-Lock</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>优化自旋锁</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 12, 2026 / 02:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49670699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49670699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49670699'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Details practical strategies to reduce spin-lock contention and latency, including back-off schemes and tuning for modern CPU architectures. The core takeaway is that small changes can yield measurable concurrency gains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍降低自旋锁竞争和延迟的实用策略，包括退避机制和对现代 CPU 架构的调优。核心是小改动也能带来显著的并发性能提升。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective post discusses code quality and testing implications with the CRAP metric from 2011. It highlights how maintainability, readability, and testability impact software reliability and the importance of addressing systemic quality issues early.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾性文章以2011年的CRAP指标为例，探讨代码质量与测试的关系，强调可维护性、可读性和可测试性对软件可靠性的影响，以及及早解决系统性质量问题的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49735541' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(352) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/ctenophores-arent-just-beautiful-theyre-biological-wonders-20260916/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ctenophores: Wonders of Biology</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>梳状水母：生物学的奇迹</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 18:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49735541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49735541' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49735541'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Comb jellies are more than pretty specimens—they’re biology wonders with unique adhesive mechanisms, nerve nets, and rapid habitat adaptation. The piece profiles these organisms as a window into early animal evolution and a source of ideas for bio-inspired technologies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>梳状水母不仅美丽，它们具备独特的黏附机制、神经网和快速的适应能力，堪称生物学奇迹。文章把它们视为窥探早期动物进化的窗口，并为仿生技术提供灵感。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2042/09/ImageGallery_CombJellies-crAlexanderSemenov_AquatilisExpedition-Social.webp' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2042/09/ImageGallery_CombJellies-crAlexanderSemenov_AquatilisExpedition-Social.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(353) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gregorygundersen.com/blog/2026/09/20/hierarchy-of-money/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Hierarchy of Money</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>货币的层级结构</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 20, 2026 / 12:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49779253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49779253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49779253'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how money is structured and created across central banks, sovereign currency, and private credit networks. The piece frames monetary hierarchies as critical infrastructure that shapes policy, incentives, and technology-enabled finance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨货币的层级结构，涵盖中央银行、主权货币与私人信贷网络等不同层级及其相互关系。作者指出这一结构对政策、激励与科技金融架构具有决定性影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 16:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49771112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49771112' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49771112'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The largest wildlife overpass in North America reduced wildlife-vehicle collisions by 91%, illustrating the effectiveness of large-scale crossings and informing future infrastructure planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>北美最大的野生动物跨越公路大幅降低野生动物与车辆碰撞率达 91%，展示了大型跨越设施的成效，并为未来基础设施规划提供参考。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49715408' data-tags='Programming,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49715408' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lemire.me/blog/2026/09/15/subnormal-floating-point-numbers-are-expensive-on-intel-processors/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Subnormal floating-point numbers are expensive on Intel processors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>次规范浮点数在 Intel 处理器上成本很高</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 15, 2026 / 09:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49715408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49715408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49715408'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Subnormal (denormal) floating-point numbers incur performance penalties on Intel CPUs due to processor microarchitecture and handling of underflow. The note explains practical implications for numerical code, suggesting normalizing data, using flush-to-zero modes, or algorithmic changes to avoid subnormals. Developers optimizing numerical workloads should consider this.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>次规范浮点数在 Intel 处理器上会带来性能开销，原因与处理器微架构对下溢的处理有关。建议对数值代码进行归一化、启用 FTZ（清零）模式或通过算法改进来避免次规范数，以优化数值工作负载。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49684042' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49684042' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(388) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xJzrU38pGWc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Paul A. M. Dirac, Interview by Friedrich Hund (1982) [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>保罗·A·M·狄拉克：弗里德里希·洪德（1982）访谈视频</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 06:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684042' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49684042' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49684042'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A rare archival video captures Paul A. M. Dirac in conversation with Friedrich Hund, offering insights into foundational quantum mechanics ideas and Dirac&#x27;s perspectives on physics. The footage provides historical context for students and researchers studying 20th-century theoretical physics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>珍贵的存档视频记录了保罗·A·M·狄拉克与弗里德里希·洪德的对谈，揭示了量子力学基础思想及狄拉克对物理学的看法。该片为学习20世纪理论物理的学生与研究者提供了宝贵的历史背景。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49736823' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49736823' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(389) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pnas.org/doi/10.1073/pnas.2201337119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Communication by means of modulated Johnson noise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过调制约翰逊噪声进行通信</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 22:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49736823' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49736823' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49736823'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Johnson noise modulation proposes a novel physical-layer communication channel leveraging thermal noise to carry information. Experiments show data transfer without conventional signaling, with potential implications for covert channels and nanoscale communications, though practical limits remain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>调制约翰逊噪声的通信提出一种利用热噪声承载信息的新型物理层通道。实验显示在不依赖常规信号的情况下也能传输数据，对隐蔽通道与纳尺度通信具有潜在意义，但实际应用仍存在局限。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(394) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/09/16/science/trex-dinosaur-temperature-warm-blooded.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>T. Rex Had a Body Temperature of 97°F</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>霸王龙体温约97华氏度</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 10:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49743691' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49743691' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49743691'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research suggests T. rex had a body temperature around 97°F (about 36°C), hinting at a warmer-blooded physiology and higher metabolic rate than traditional cold-blooded models. The finding reshapes debates about dinosaur biology and behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究显示霸王龙体温约为华氏97度（约36°C），提示其具备较高的新陈代谢和偏暖血生理。此发现重新推动了关于恐龙生物学与行为的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The public opening reveals the largest known Roman mosaic unearthed beneath the Baths of Trajan, offering a vivid glimpse into imperial-era art. The find enriches the narrative around Rome’s public baths and decorative programs.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(397) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cacm.acm.org/blogcacm/why-is-privacy-so-hard/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is privacy so hard? (2019)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>隐私为何如此难？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 14:13 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49688766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49688766' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49688766'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Privacy is hard due to tangled data ecosystems, network effects, and misaligned incentives across platforms. The piece argues for privacy-by-design, pragmatic regulation, and user-centric approaches to make privacy workable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>隐私之所以难，是因为数据生态、网络效应与平台激励错位叠加。文章主张在设计、监管与用户为中心的取向上协同，才能让隐私在现实世界中变得可行。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(404) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://joecmarshall.com/posts/vibecoding-is-the-new-internet-dating/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vibe Coding is the new Internet Dating?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vibe Coding: 情感编码，是新一代互联网约会吗？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 14, 2026 / 18:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49706708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49706708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49706708'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vibe Coding explores using social or emotional signals as part of matching in online dating, potentially shifting focus from profiles to compatibility cues. The idea raises questions about privacy, bias, and how much weight should be given to subjective vibes in tech-mediated dating.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vibe Coding 探讨在网络约会中将情感信号作为匹配要素，可能将焦点从个人资料转向兼容性线索。该概念引发隐私、偏见以及在技术媒介的约会中应赋予主观“气质感”多少权重的讨论。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49660791' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(406) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://clickhouse.com/blog/introducing-walshadow' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WalShadow: Sub-second Postgres replication to ClickHouse from physical WAL</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WalShadow：从物理WAL实现子秒级Postgres到ClickHouse的复制</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 11, 2026 / 09:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49660791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49660791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49660791'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WalShadow enables near real-time replication from PostgreSQL to ClickHouse by streaming physical WAL changes, enabling analytics across PostgreSQL transactions with minimal lag. It showcases how database-to-analytic pipelines can be kept synchronized efficiently and may influence future replication tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WalShadow 通过流式传输物理WAL，将PostgreSQL的变更实时复制到ClickHouse，几乎实现子秒级的延迟。该方案展示了将事务性数据库与分析数据库保持近实时同步的高效路径，可能推动未来的复制工具演进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://clickhouse.com/_next/image?url=%2Fuploads%2Fwalshadow_sep2026_banner_6dbc425dd9.jpg&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;q=75' data-full='https://clickhouse.com/_next/image?url=%2Fuploads%2Fwalshadow_sep2026_banner_6dbc425dd9.jpg&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;q=75' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(411) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/how-apple-detectives-solved-the-mystery-of-an-ancient-tree-and-rewrote-the-history-of-fruit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple detectives solved mystery of ancient tree and rewrote the history of fruit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果侦探解开古树之谜，改写水果历史</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 04:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49725404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49725404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49725404'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Genetic analysis of an ancient apple tree rewrites assumptions about domestication and fruit history, shedding light on how human selection shaped modern varieties. The finding blends botany, archaeology, and DNA for fresh agricultural insights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对一棵古老苹果树的基因分析推翻了关于栽培史的既有假设，揭示人类选择如何影响现代品种的起源与演化。该发现将植物学、考古学与 DNA 研究结合起来，带来新的农业见解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/aa531cd7-0f51-4ee2-93bf-676c480125cb/saw1026McKe01.jpg?m=1788461288.529&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/aa531cd7-0f51-4ee2-93bf-676c480125cb/saw1026McKe01.jpg?m=1788461288.529&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49757493' data-tags='Data,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49757493' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(414) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://victoriaritvo.com/blog/predicting-survivor/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Model for Winning Survivor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>赢得幸存者的模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 10:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49757493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49757493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49757493'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes a data-driven framework for predicting Survivor winners by quantifying social capital, alliance strength, and performance in challenges, then simulating vote dynamics. The takeaway is that social mechanics can dominate raw performance, and even simple models can forecast outcomes under uncertainty.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一个以数据驱动的框架来预测《幸存者》冠军，通过量化社交资本、联盟强度以及挑战表现来模拟投票过程。核心结论是，社交博弈往往比体能更决定胜负，即使在不确定环境下，简单模型也能给出相对可行的胜出概率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://victoriaritvo.com/blog/predicting-survivor/survivor_beach.jpg' data-full='https://victoriaritvo.com/blog/predicting-survivor/survivor_beach.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flock 在客户流失时提供员工自愿离职方案</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 19:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49762835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49762835' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49762835'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Facing customer churn, Flock is offering voluntary buyouts to employees to reduce costs and navigate cash burn. The move signals investor pressure and the startup&#x27;s struggle to retain enterprise customers amid a shifting security tooling market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在客户流失压力下，Flock 提供自愿离职补偿以减低成本并度过现金消耗期。此举反映了投资者压力以及在不断变化的安全工具市场中留住企业客户的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(416) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.coredump.cx/p/inverse-square-law' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Inverse-Square Law</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>反平方定律</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 21:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49692098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49692098' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49692098'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Inverse-Square Law examines how a simple 1/r^2 relationship governs many physical and information systems, offering intuition for scaling, propagation, and interaction. The piece connects this principle to practical engineering insights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>反平方定律探讨简单的 1/r^2 关系如何支配众多物理与信息系统，为放大、传播和相互作用提供直觉。文章将这一原理与工程实操联系起来。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Ozul!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F16863eb6-57e3-4297-9efe-21831af77c4f_1600x900.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Ozul!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F16863eb6-57e3-4297-9efe-21831af77c4f_1600x900.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(417) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thelastsoftwareengineer.substack.com/p/from-stonemasons-to-carpenters' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>From Stonemasons to Carpenters</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49743490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49743490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49743490'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>From Stonemasons to Carpenters uses a craftsman metaphor to trace the evolution of software engineering. It argues that attention to craft, tooling, and discipline persists as software practices mature.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以工匠精神为线索，探讨软件工程的演进：从传统手艺到现代工程的体系化实践。强调注重做事的细致与工具链成熟度在当代软件开发中的作用。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新发现的野猫物种，现存仅一只</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 00:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49751222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49751222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49751222'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Biologists announce the discovery of a new wild cat species, known from a single surviving individual. The finding highlights challenges in species discovery, conservation genetics, and the fragility of rare taxa. It underscores the importance of protecting habitats even when only a sliver of information exists.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>生物学家宣布发现一种新的野猫物种，目前仅有一只已知存活个体。这一发现凸显物种发现、保护遗传学以及稀有物种脆弱性的挑战，强调在信息有限时也要保护栖息地。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49699590' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49699590' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(420) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.amazon.science/blog/when-llm-judges-agree-should-we-believe-them' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When LLM judges agree, should we believe them?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当大型语言模型评判一致时，我们应该相信它们吗？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 14, 2026 / 09:29 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49699590' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49699590' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49699590'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>When LLMs reach agreement on judgments, reliability and calibration come into question. This piece probes whether consensus among models should be treated as trustworthy signal or be treated with skepticism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>当大模型在判断上达成一致时，可靠性与校准问题就会凸显；文章探讨是否应将模型之间的共识视为可信信号，还是应保持警惕。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.amazon.science/dims4/default/116a8a4/2147483647/strip/true/crop/1228x645+0+23/resize/1200x630!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Famzn-science-production-science.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com%2Fscience%2F70%2F22%2F5afbc05541c3bcdcf18bbfb49620%2Fagreement-fig1.png' data-full='https://cdn.amazon.science/dims4/default/116a8a4/2147483647/strip/true/crop/1228x645+0+23/resize/1200x630!/quality/90/?url=https%3A%2F%2Famzn-science-production-science.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com%2Fscience%2F70%2F22%2F5afbc05541c3bcdcf18bbfb49620%2Fagreement-fig1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(425) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danluu.com/exercise-7/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bad benchmarks and evals: Senior SWE-Bench, napkin math, and winter tires</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>糟糕的基准与评估：Senior SWE‑Bench、纸笔估算与冬季轮胎</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 11, 2026 / 02:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49655621' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49655621' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49655621'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post critiques common benchmarks and estimation practices in software engineering, arguing that many metrics mislead about real impact. It uses examples like &#x27;Senior SWE-Bench&#x27; and &#x27;napkin math&#x27; to show how sloppy numbers distort decisions and offer guidance for more robust evaluation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇博文批评了软件工程中常见的基准与估算方法，指出诸多度量会误导对真实影响的判断。通过“Senior SWE‑Bench”和“napkin math”等示例，揭示粗糙数字如何扭曲决策，并给出更严谨的评估思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://danluu.com/images/exercise-7/deep-swe.webp' data-full='https://danluu.com/images/exercise-7/deep-swe.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(426) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eterdb.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: EterDB, a Postgres fork that makes it easy to recover from incidents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：EterDB，一个让事故恢复更容易的 Postgres 分支</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 10, 2026 / 08:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49645654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49645654' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49645654'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EterDB is a Postgres fork designed to simplify incident recovery, enabling faster restoration to a known-good state after outages. It emphasizes features that streamline disaster recovery and reduce MTTR, appealing to teams that need reliable data resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EterDB 是一个基于 Postgres 的分支，旨在简化事故恢复，在停机后更快地回滚到已知良好状态。它强调提升灾难恢复效率、降低平均修复时间，适合需要高可靠性数据韧性的团队。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Scry，可编程的互联网搜索，带拥塞定价</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 16:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49748041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49748041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 18</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49748041'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scry offers programmable web search with congestion pricing, enabling demand-based cost controls for API usage and scaling. The approach could incentivize smarter search usage and provide predictable costs for apps reliant on large-scale queries. It signals experimentation with monetization models in the search domain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Scry 提供带拥塞定价的可编程网络搜索，按需定价以控制 API 使用与扩展成本，帮助应用在大规模查询时实现可预测开支。这种模式或将推动更高效的搜索调用，并带来对搜索领域定价模型的创新探索。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49712409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49712409' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic&#x27;s co-founder argues that an AI kill switch may need to be mandatory, framing safety as a governance issue. The take is part of a broader debate about mandatory fail-safes, oversight, and the trade-offs of such controls.</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49691584'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A data breach at Chess.com exposed about 7.3 million users, with indications that the data was scraped rather than stolen from a database. The incident highlights the privacy risks of large gaming platforms and the need for stronger API protections and rate limiting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chess.com 的一次数据泄露暴露约730万用户，证据指向通过数据抓取而非直接数据库被盗。事件凸显大型游戏平台的隐私风险，并呼吁加强 API 保护与访问速率限制。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巨型恐龙无法坐在它们的蛋上</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 17:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49690084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49690084' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49690084'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New paleontology findings about titanosaurs show their eggs were so large that adults likely couldn&#x27;t incubate by sitting on them, suggesting alternative nesting or incubation strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于巨型恐龙的新研究表明，它们的蛋太大，成年动物不太可能蹲坐孵化，因此推断出可能采用替代的巢育或孵化方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OEMpocalypse：未授权的 Android 应用在三星、小米等设备上获取 root 权限</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 13, 2026 / 19:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49691152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49691152' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49691152'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OEMpocalypse demonstrates an attack path where an unprivileged Android app could root devices on several manufacturers, illustrating dangerous privilege-escalation risks tied to OEM unlocking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OEMpocalypse 展示了一个漏洞路径：未获得特权的安卓应用可能在多家厂商设备上获得 root 权限，揭示了与厂商解锁相关的高危特权提升风险。</p>
