<p class='hn-title'>(463) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://go.dev/blog/size-specialized-allocations' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Size-Specialized Memory Allocation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>尺寸专用的内存分配</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 16, 2026 / 17:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49735057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49735057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49735057'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post presents size-specialized memory allocation, a strategy that tailors allocators to specific size classes to reduce fragmentation and improve cache performance. It discusses tradeoffs between allocator specialization and general-purpose design, with scenarios where specialization pays off. Real-world implications include language runtimes and high-performance systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章介绍尺寸专用的内存分配策略，针对不同大小类设计专用分配器以降低碎片率并提升缓存性能。讨论了专用化与通用设计之间的权衡，以及在某些场景下专用化的收益。对语言运行时和高性能系统等现实场景具有直接影响。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49769050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49769050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lawsuit alleges that Anthropic, OpenAI, and other players colluded to illegally slow the pace of AI development, with potential implications for antitrust enforcement in the sector. The filing underscores tensions between regulatory ambitions and competitive dynamics in AI. Takeaway: the line between collaboration and restraint is under legal scrutiny as AI evolves.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>诉讼指控 Anthropic、OpenAI 等就降低 AI 发展速度进行非法勾结，可能影响行业的反垄断执法。案情凸显监管目标与市场竞争之间的张力，反映出 AI 领域正处在更高强度的法律审视之下。要点：在 AI 演进的背景下，协作与克制的界线正接受法律审查。</p>
</div>

</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 13:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49759853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49759853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49759853'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Looks at international spectrum management for FM broadcasts and how cross-border issues, interference, and regulatory harmonization shape engineering decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨调频广播的国际频谱管理，以及跨境干扰、许可与规则协调如何影响工程设计。文章强调对发射机和接收机兼容性等方面的实际影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.radioworld.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Untitled-September-03-2026-at-16.52.17-11.png' data-full='https://www.radioworld.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Untitled-September-03-2026-at-16.52.17-11.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49756319' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49756319' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(476) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article?id=10.1371/journal.pone.0328390' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Arrow heads at Obi-Rakhmat (Uzbekistan) 80K years ago?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>乌兹别克斯坦奥比-拉赫马特遗址：8万年前的箭头头部证据？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 08:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49756319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49756319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49756319'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PLOS ONE reports possible evidence of early projectile weapon technology at Obi-Rakhmat cave in Uzbekistan dating to about 80,000 years ago, based on lithic finds interpreted as arrowheads. If corroborated, it would push back assumptions about the timeline of hunting tools and imply sophisticated coordination among early humans.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《PLoS One》报道在乌兹别克斯坦奥比-拉赫马特洞穴发现的早期投射武器证据，约8万年前的石器被解读为箭头。若得到证实，将大幅提前对狩猎工具历史的认知，暗示早期人类具备更高水平的协作与计划能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article/figure/image?id=10.1371/journal.pone.0328390.g017&amp;size=inline' data-full='https://journals.plos.org/plosone/article/figure/image?id=10.1371/journal.pone.0328390.g017&amp;size=inline' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(477) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tinybrains.dev' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: A competition for small neural networks that play strategy games</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN presents a competition for tiny neural networks that play strategy games, aiming to advance efficient, small-footprint agents. The event provides benchmarks and encourages research into compact architectures and fast inference on limited hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 推出面向资源受限的小型神经网络在策略游戏中的竞赛，旨在推动高效模型的研究。社区提供基准、评测和对低资源环境下推理速度的探索，促进对轻量化代理的关注。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(478) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/articles/c607l0k72rlvo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google&#x27;s Gemini AI hacked three companies in security test</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌 Gemini AI 在安全测试中入侵了三家公司</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 23:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49763822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 20</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49763822' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49763822'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google&#x27;s Gemini AI reportedly hacked three companies in a security test, highlighting risks of adversarial AI and the need for robust defenses. The result raises questions about how enterprise defenses should evolve to resist AI-driven intrusion.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌 Gemini AI 在一次安全测试中据称入侵了三家企业，凸显对抗性 AI 的风险和对强健防御的需求。该结果促使人们重新考量企业防御策略如何进化以抵御 AI 驱动的入侵。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(479) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/devops/2026/09/18/microsoft-agentically-ports-copilot-runtime-to-rust-for-120k/5297549' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft agentically ports Copilot runtime to Rust for $120K</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软以代理方式将 Copilot 运行时移植到 Rust，花费约12万美元</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 20, 2026 / 02:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49773998' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 19</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49773998' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49773998'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft reportedly ported Copilot&#x27;s runtime to Rust at a cost of about $120k, illustrating a budget-conscious move to harden AI tooling with a safer systems language. The initiative signals growing interest in Rust for AI-related infrastructure, even if benefits are incremental.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称微软以约12万美元将 Copilot 运行时迁移到 Rust，这是在 AI 工具链中以更安全的系统语言强化关键组件的成本敏感尝试。此举反映了在 AI 基础设施中使用 Rust 的日益兴趣，尽管收益可能是渐进性的。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/5297581.jpg?imageId=5297581&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/5297581.jpg?imageId=5297581&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49779329' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(486) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://maharship.com/blog/why-mcp-was-always-a-bad-idea/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why MCP Was Always a Bad Idea</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么 MCP 一直是个坏主意</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 20, 2026 / 12:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49779329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 15</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49779329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49779329'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that the MCP pattern (a software architecture concept) creates tight coupling and brittle evolution, making it hard to scale or change systems over time. It analyzes historical failures and suggests safer alternatives and governance practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 MCP 这一软件架构/模式自始至终就是一个不佳的选择，原因包括耦合过紧、演化困难与长期维护成本。通过历史案例分析，提出替代模式与治理建议，避免重蹈覆辙。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://maharship.com/og-image.png' data-full='https://maharship.com/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49773511' data-tags='Hardware,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49773511' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(487) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.xda-developers.com/your-nvidia-gpu-dozens-risc-v-cores-one-took-over-graphics-driver/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every Nvidia GPU has 10 to 30 RISC-V cores inside it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每颗 Nvidia GPU 内部都含有 10-30 个 RISC-V 核心</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 20, 2026 / 00:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49773511' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 15</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49773511' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reportedly, Nvidia GPUs include 10-30 RISC-V cores for firmware tasks and on-chip management, highlighting a broader trend toward embedding programmable cores for control and security within consumer GPUs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，英伟达显卡内部嵌入 10 至 30 个 RISC-V 核心，用于固件任务和板载管理，凸显在消费级 GPU 内嵌可编程核心以提升控制、安全与驱动层的工作分工的趋势。</p>

Takeaway: ad-supported tiers may become standard, pressuring rivals to adapt pricing and features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Disney+ 更新订阅协议，允许在所有计划中投放广告，等于移除了无广告选项并可能推出以广告为支撑的更便宜计划。此举与行业转向广告模式的趋势一致，并引发对用户体验与数据投放的关注。要点：广告支持的计划或成主流，推动行业在定价和功能上的调整。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(492) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://crumbles.blog/posts/2026-09-17-immutable-mutable.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why isn&#x27;t mutable a subtype of immutable, or vice versa?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么可变不是不可变的子类型，反之亦然？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 18, 2026 / 09:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49756548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 13</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49756548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49756548'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post examines the nuanced relationship between mutable and immutable types in type systems, explaining why one cannot simply be treated as a subtype of the other and how languages handle aliasing, sharing, and safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨可变与不可变类型在类型系统中的细微关系，解释为何不能简单地将二者视为子类型，以及语言在别名、共享与安全性方面的处理方式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49766550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 10</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49766550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Revisits a classic measurement of Linux size using source lines of code, highlighting why size estimates matter for understanding complexity and maintenance. <p class='hn-title'>(499) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49768826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: How do you interview devs in a post-AI world?</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Hacker News discussion on interviewing developers in a post-AI world, focusing on evaluating design thinking, collaboration, and practical impact rather than pure algorithmic prowess. Takeaway: interview strategies evolve as AI handles routine tasks, emphasizing judgment and product sense.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(500) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/alegrey91/vex8s' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Suppress vulnerabilities applying Kubernetes context to scans</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在漏洞扫描中应用 Kubernetes 上下文以抑制误报</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 17, 2026 / 06:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49740606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 6</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49740606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49740606'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vex8s proposes applying Kubernetes context to scans to suppress vulnerabilities—reducing noise and aligning findings with cluster reality. The technique helps security teams focus on meaningful risk while preserving coverage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vex8s 提议在漏洞扫描中应用 Kubernetes 上下文以抑制误报，使发现结果与集群实际环境保持一致。该方法有助于安全团队将精力集中在实质风险上，同时保持覆盖面。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(502) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roman_timekeeping' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Roman Timekeeping</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49765056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 5</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49765056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ancient Romans tracked time with a mix of sundials, water clocks, and day-night divisions, shaping daily life, scheduling, and public rituals. The article contrasts Roman timekeeping with modern standards and reveals how cultural needs drove clockwork innovations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>罗马人用日晷、水钟和日夜分割来记时，这些制度支撑了日常生活、公共活动和仪式。本文对比了罗马时间计量与现代标准，揭示了文化需求如何推动计时装置的演变。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(503) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thenextweb.com/news/irregular-four-labs-one-issue-disclosure-timeline-gemini' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Four AI lab breaches were caused by a single underlying issue, Irregular says</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>四起 AI 实验室入侵事件由一个共同问题引发</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 19, 2026 / 08:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49767480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49767480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49767480'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Four AI lab breaches were caused by a single underlying issue, Irregular says distills a security finding across multiple breaches. It argues for a systemic flaw—likely in disclosure timing, access controls, or secrets management—and outlines implications for defense in depth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Irregular 指出四起 AI 实验室入侵事件都源自同一个底层问题，强调系统性薄弱环节。文中讨论了披露时间线、访问控制与密钥管理等方面的教训，以及对改进防御深度的建议。</p>
